256 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node node, Node parent) {
257 |       Node nameNode = null;
258 |       switch (node.getType()) {
259 |         case Token.NAME:
260 |           nameNode = node;
261 |           break;
262 |         case Token.VAR:
263 |           if (node.hasOneChild()) {
264 |             nameNode = node.getFirstChild();
265 |           }
266 |           break;
267 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
268 |           nameNode = node.getFirstChild();
269 |           break;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (nameNode != null) {
273 |         JSDocInfo info = node.getJSDocInfo();
274 |         if (info != null && info.hasEnumParameterType()) {
275 |           registry.identifyEnumName(nameNode.getQualifiedName());
276 |         }

===== M0007 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferThisType(JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 337-371
Documentation:
/**
   * Infers the type of {@code this}.
   * @param info The JSDocInfo for this function.
   * @param owner The node for the object whose prototype "owns" this function.
   *     For example, {@code A} in the expression {@code A.prototype.foo}. May
   *     be null to indicate that this is not a prototype property.
   */
Method code:
337 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferThisType(JSDocInfo info,
338 |       @Nullable Node owner) {
339 |     ObjectType maybeThisType = null;
340 |     if (info != null && info.hasThisType()) {
341 |       maybeThisType = ObjectType.cast(
342 |           info.getThisType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
343 |     }
344 |     if (maybeThisType != null) {
345 |       // TODO(user): Doing an instanceof check here is too
346 |       // restrictive as (Date,Error) is, for instance, an object type
347 |       // even though its implementation is a UnionType. Would need to
348 |       // create interfaces JSType, ObjectType, FunctionType etc and have
349 |       // separate implementation instead of the class hierarchy, so that
350 |       // union types can also be object types, etc.
351 |       thisType = maybeThisType;
352 |     } else if (owner != null &&
353 |                (info == null || !info.hasType())) {
354 |       // If the function is of the form:
355 |       // x.prototype.y = function() {}
356 |       // then we can assume "x" is the @this type. On the other hand,
357 |       // if it's of the form:
358 |       // /** @type {Function} */ x.prototype.y;
359 |       // then we should not give it a @this type.
360 |       String ownerTypeName = owner.getQualifiedName();
361 |       ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(
362 |           typeRegistry.getType(
363 |               scope, ownerTypeName, sourceName,
364 |               owner.getLineno(), owner.getCharno()));
365 |       if (ownerType != null) {
366 |         thisType = ownerType;
367 |       }
368 |     }
369 | 
370 |     return this;
371 |   }

===== M0014 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken,WhitespaceOption)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1103-1200
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the text found on the current line and all subsequent
   * until either an annotation, end of comment or end of file is reached.
   * Note that if this method detects an end of line as the first token, it
   * will quit immediately (indicating that there is no text where it was
   * expected).  Note that token = info.token; should be called after this
   * method is used to update the token properly in the parser.
   *
   * @param token The start token.
   * @param option How to handle whitespace.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1103 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
1104 |   private ExtractionInfo extractMultilineTextualBlock(JsDocToken token,
1105 |                                                       WhitespaceOption option) {
1106 | 
1107 |     if (token == JsDocToken.EOC || token == JsDocToken.EOL ||
1108 |         token == JsDocToken.EOF) {
1109 |       return new ExtractionInfo("", token);
1110 |     }
1111 | 
1112 |     stream.update();
1113 |     int startLineno = stream.getLineno();
1114 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno() + 1;
1115 | 
1116 |     // Read the content from the first line.
1117 |     String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1118 | 
1119 |     if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1120 |       line = line.trim();
1121 |     }
1122 | 
1123 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1124 |     builder.append(line);
1125 | 
1126 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1127 |     token = next();
1128 | 
1129 |     boolean ignoreStar = false;
1130 | 
1131 |     do {
1132 |       switch (token) {
1133 |         case STAR:
1134 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1135 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1136 |               builder.append(' ');
1137 |             }
1138 | 
1139 |             builder.append('*');
1140 |           }
1141 | 
1142 |           token = next();
1143 |           continue;
1144 | 
1145 |         case EOL:
1146 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.SINGLE_LINE) {
1147 |             builder.append("\n");
1148 |           }
1149 | 
1150 |           ignoreStar = true;
1151 |           token = next();
1152 |           continue;
1153 | 
1154 |         case ANNOTATION:
1155 |         case EOC:
1156 |         case EOF:
1157 |           // When we're capturing a license block, annotations
1158 |           // in the block are ok.
1159 |           if (!(option == WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE &&
1160 |                 token == JsDocToken.ANNOTATION)) {
1161 |             String multilineText = builder.toString();
1162 | 
1163 |             if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1164 |               multilineText = multilineText.trim();
1165 |             }
1166 | 
1167 |             int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1168 |             int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1169 | 
1170 |             if (multilineText.length() > 0) {
1171 |               jsdocBuilder.markText(multilineText, startLineno, startCharno,
1172 |                   endLineno, endCharno);
1173 |             }
1174 | 
1175 |             return new ExtractionInfo(multilineText, token);
1176 |           }
1177 | 
1178 |           // FALL THROUGH
1179 | 
1180 |         default:
1181 |           ignoreStar = false;
1182 |           state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
1183 | 
1184 |           if (builder.length() > 0) {
1185 |             builder.append(' ');
1186 |           }
1187 | 
1188 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1189 | 
1190 |           line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1191 | 
1192 |           if (option != WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE) {
1193 |             line = trimEnd(line);
1194 |           }
1195 | 
1196 |           builder.append(line);
1197 |           token = next();
1198 |       }
1199 |     } while (true);
1200 |   }

===== M0015 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.build()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 149-175
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a union.
   * @return A UnionType if it has two or more alternates, the
   *    only alternate if it has one and otherwise {@code NO_TYPE}.
   */
Method code:
149 |   JSType build() {
150 |     if (result == null) {
151 |       if (isAllType) {
152 |         result = registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
153 |       } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
154 |         if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
155 |           result = registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
156 |         } else {
157 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
158 |         }
159 |       } else {
160 |         int size = alternates.size();
161 |         if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
162 |           result = registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
163 |         } else {
164 |           if (size > 1) {
165 |             result = new UnionType(registry, getAlternateListCopy());
166 |           } else if (size == 1) {
167 |             result = alternates.iterator().next();
168 |           } else {
169 |             result = registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
170 |           }
171 |         }
172 |       }
173 |     }
174 |     return result;
175 |   }

===== M0017 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 148-171
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
148 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
149 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
150 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
151 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
152 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
153 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
154 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
155 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
156 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
157 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
158 |           return false;
159 |         }
160 |       } else {
161 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
162 |           return false;
163 |         }
164 |       }
165 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
166 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
167 |     }
168 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
169 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
170 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
171 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSDocString(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 281-305
Documentation:

Method code:
281 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
282 |     switch (c) {
283 |       case '@':
284 |       case '*':
285 |       case ',':
286 |       case '>':
287 |       case ':':
288 |       case '(':
289 |       case ')':
290 |       case '{':
291 |       case '}':
292 |       case '[':
293 |       case ']':
294 |       case '?':
295 |       case '!':
296 |       case '|':
297 |       case '=':
298 |       case EOF_CHAR:
299 |       case '\n':
300 |         return false;
301 | 
302 |       default:
303 |         return !isJSSpace(c);
304 |     }
305 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)

Original range: 1301-1318
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpressionAnnotation := TypeExpression |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   */
Method code:
1301 |   private Node parseTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1302 |     if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1303 |       skipEOLs();
1304 |       Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(next());
1305 |       if (typeNode != null) {
1306 |         skipEOLs();
1307 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1308 |           reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1309 |         } else {
1310 |           next();
1311 |         }
1312 |       }
1313 | 
1314 |       return typeNode;
1315 |     } else {
1316 |       return parseTypeExpression(token);
1317 |     }
1318 |   }

369 |     JSType getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(String sourceName,

373 |           node.getFirstChild() : null;
374 |       if (info != null) {
375 |         if (info.hasType()) {
376 |           jsType = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
377 |         } else if (FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info)) {
378 |           String fnName = node.getQualifiedName();
379 | 
380 |           // constructors are often handled separately.
381 |           if (info.isConstructor() && typeRegistry.getType(fnName) != null) {
382 |             return null;
383 |           }

397 |                 objNode.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {

===== M0076 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferInheritance(JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 273-316
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
273 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
274 |     if (info != null) {
275 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
276 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
277 | 
278 |       // base type
279 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {
280 |         if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
281 |           baseType = ObjectType.cast(info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
282 |           if (baseType == null) {
283 |             reportWarning(EXTENDS_NON_OBJECT, fnName, baseType.toString());
284 |           }
285 |         } else {
286 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
287 |         }
288 |       }
289 | 
290 |       // implemented interfaces
291 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
292 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
293 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
294 |           ObjectType interType = ObjectType.cast(t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry));
295 |           if (interType != null) {
296 |             implementedInterfaces.add(interType);
297 |           } else {
298 |             reportError(BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, fnName);
299 |           }
300 |         }
301 |         if (baseType != null) {
302 |           JSType maybeFunctionType = baseType.getConstructor();
303 |           if (maybeFunctionType instanceof FunctionType) {
304 |             FunctionType functionType = baseType.getConstructor();
305 |             Iterables.addAll(
306 |                 implementedInterfaces,
307 |                 functionType.getImplementedInterfaces());
308 |           }
309 |         }
310 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
311 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
312 |       }
313 |     }
314 | 
315 |     return this;
316 |   }

===== M0078 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 162-213
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
162 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
163 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
164 |     if (oldType == null) {
165 |       return this;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
169 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
170 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
171 |       // Not a function literal.
172 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
173 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
174 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
175 |       }
176 |     } else {
177 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
178 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
179 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
180 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
181 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
182 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
183 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
184 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
185 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
186 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
187 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
188 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
189 | 
190 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
191 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
192 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
193 | 
194 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
195 |           // arguments.
196 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
197 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
198 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
199 |           }
200 |         } else {
201 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
202 |               paramBuilder,
203 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
204 |               warnedAboutArgList,
205 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
206 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
207 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
208 |         }
209 |       }
210 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
211 |     }
212 |     return this;
213 |   }

===== M0080 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1488-1513
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1488 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1489 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1490 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1491 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1492 |       skipEOLs();
1493 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1494 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1495 |       skipEOLs();
1496 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1497 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1498 |       skipEOLs();
1499 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1500 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1501 |       String string = stream.getString();
1502 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1503 |         skipEOLs();
1504 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1505 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1506 |         return newStringNode(string);
1507 |       } else {
1508 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1509 |       }
1510 |     }
1511 | 
1512 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1513 |   }

===== M0084 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.getFunctionType(String,Node,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 564-641
Documentation:
/**
     * Gets the function type from the function node and its attached
     * {@link JSDocInfo}.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param rValue the function node. It must be a {@link Token#FUNCTION}.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
564 |     private FunctionType getFunctionType(String name,
565 |         Node rValue, JSDocInfo info, @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
566 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
567 | 
568 |       // Handle function aliases.
569 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName()) {
570 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
571 |         if (var != null && var.getType() instanceof FunctionType) {
572 |           functionType = (FunctionType) var.getType();
573 |           if (functionType != null && functionType.isConstructor()) {
574 |             typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
575 |           }
576 |         }
577 |         return functionType;
578 |       }
579 | 
580 |       Node owner = null;
581 |       if (lvalueNode != null) {
582 |         owner = getPrototypePropertyOwner(lvalueNode);
583 |       }
584 | 
585 |       Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
586 |       boolean isFnLiteral =
587 |           rValue != null && rValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION;
588 |       Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
589 |       Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
590 |           rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
591 |       Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
592 | 
593 |       if (functionType == null && info != null && info.hasType()) {
594 |         JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
595 | 
596 |         // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
597 |         type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
598 |         if (type.isFunctionType()) {
599 |           functionType = (FunctionType) type;
600 |           functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
601 |         }
602 |       }
603 | 
604 |       if (functionType == null) {
605 |         // Find the type of any overridden function.
606 |         FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
607 |         if (lvalueNode != null && lvalueNode.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
608 |             lvalueNode.isQualifiedName()) {
609 |           Var var = scope.getVar(
610 |               lvalueNode.getFirstChild().getQualifiedName());
611 |           if (var != null) {
612 |             ObjectType ownerType = ObjectType.cast(var.getType());
613 |             if (ownerType != null) {
614 |               String propName = lvalueNode.getLastChild().getString();
615 |               overriddenPropType = findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
616 |             }
617 |           }
618 |         }
619 | 
620 |         functionType =
621 |             new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
622 |                 scope)
623 |             .setSourceNode(fnRoot)
624 |             .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
625 |             .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
626 |             .inferReturnType(info)
627 |             .inferInheritance(info)
628 |             .inferThisType(info, owner)
629 |             .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
630 |             .inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(fnBlock)
631 |             .buildAndRegister();
632 |       }
633 | 
634 |       // assigning the function type to the function node
635 |       if (rValue != null) {
636 |         setDeferredType(rValue, functionType);
637 |       }
638 | 
639 |       // all done
640 |       return functionType;
641 |     }

===== M0087 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 445-791
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
445 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
446 |     JSType childType;
447 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
448 |     Node left, right;
449 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
450 |     boolean typeable = true;
451 | 
452 |     switch (n.getType()) {
453 |       case Token.NAME:
454 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
455 |         break;
456 | 
457 |       case Token.LP:
458 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
459 |         // ignored here.
460 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
461 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
462 |         } else {
463 |           typeable = false;
464 |         }
465 |         break;
466 | 
467 |       case Token.COMMA:
468 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
469 |         break;
470 | 
471 |       case Token.TRUE:
472 |       case Token.FALSE:
473 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
474 |         break;
475 | 
476 |       case Token.THIS:
477 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
478 |         break;
479 | 
480 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
481 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
482 |         break;
483 | 
484 |       case Token.GET_REF:
485 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
486 |         break;
487 | 
488 |       case Token.NULL:
489 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.NUMBER:
493 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
494 |           ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
495 |         } else {
496 |           typeable = false;
497 |         }
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.STRING:
505 |         if (n.getParent().getType() != Token.OBJECTLIT) {
506 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
507 |         } else {
508 |           typeable = false;
509 |         }
510 |         break;
511 | 
512 |       case Token.REGEXP:
513 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
514 |         break;
515 | 
516 |       case Token.GETPROP:
517 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
518 |         typeable = !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
519 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
520 |         break;
521 | 
522 |       case Token.GETELEM:
523 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
524 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
525 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
526 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
527 |         typeable = false;
528 |         break;
529 | 
530 |       case Token.VAR:
531 |         visitVar(t, n);
532 |         typeable = false;
533 |         break;
534 | 
535 |       case Token.NEW:
536 |         visitNew(t, n);
537 |         typeable = true;
538 |         break;
539 | 
540 |       case Token.CALL:
541 |         visitCall(t, n);
542 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
543 |         break;
544 | 
545 |       case Token.RETURN:
546 |         visitReturn(t, n);
547 |         typeable = false;
548 |         break;
549 | 
550 |       case Token.DEC:
551 |       case Token.INC:
552 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
553 |         validator.expectNumber(
554 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
555 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
556 |         break;
557 | 
558 |       case Token.NOT:
559 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
560 |         break;
561 | 
562 |       case Token.VOID:
563 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
564 |         break;
565 | 
566 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
567 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
568 |         break;
569 | 
570 |       case Token.BITNOT:
571 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
572 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
573 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
574 |               childType.toString());
575 |         }
576 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
577 |         break;
578 | 
579 |       case Token.POS:
580 |       case Token.NEG:
581 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
582 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
583 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
584 |         break;
585 | 
586 |       case Token.EQ:
587 |       case Token.NE: {
588 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
589 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
590 | 
591 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
592 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
593 |         TernaryValue result =
594 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
595 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
596 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
597 |             result = result.not();
598 |           }
599 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
600 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
601 |         }
602 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
603 |         break;
604 |       }
605 | 
606 |       case Token.SHEQ:
607 |       case Token.SHNE: {
608 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
609 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
610 | 
611 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
612 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
613 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
614 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
615 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
616 |               rightType.toString());
617 |         }
618 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
619 |         break;
620 |       }
621 | 
622 |       case Token.LT:
623 |       case Token.LE:
624 |       case Token.GT:
625 |       case Token.GE:
626 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
627 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
628 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
629 |           validator.expectNumber(
630 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
631 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
632 |           validator.expectNumber(
633 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
634 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
635 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
636 |           // OK.
637 |         } else {
638 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
639 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
640 |           // should match a string context.
641 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
642 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
643 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
644 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
645 |           message = "right side of comparison";
646 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
647 |           validator.expectNotVoid(
648 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
649 |         }
650 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
651 |         break;
652 | 
653 |       case Token.IN:
654 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
655 |         right = n.getLastChild();
656 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
657 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
658 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
659 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
660 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
661 |         break;
662 | 
663 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
664 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
665 |         right = n.getLastChild();
666 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
667 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
668 | 
669 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
670 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
671 |         validator.expectActualObject(
672 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
673 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
674 |         break;
675 | 
676 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
677 |         visitAssign(t, n);
678 |         typeable = false;
679 |         break;
680 | 
681 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
682 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
683 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
684 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
685 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
686 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
687 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
688 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
689 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
690 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
691 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
692 |       case Token.LSH:
693 |       case Token.RSH:
694 |       case Token.URSH:
695 |       case Token.DIV:
696 |       case Token.MOD:
697 |       case Token.BITOR:
698 |       case Token.BITXOR:
699 |       case Token.BITAND:
700 |       case Token.SUB:
701 |       case Token.ADD:
702 |       case Token.MUL:
703 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
704 |         break;
705 | 
706 |       case Token.DELPROP:
707 |         if (!isReference(n.getFirstChild())) {
708 |           report(t, n, BAD_DELETE);
709 |         }
710 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
711 |         break;
712 | 
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
715 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
716 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
717 |         typeable = false;
718 |         break;
719 | 
720 |       case Token.WITH: {
721 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
722 |         childType = getJSType(child);
723 |         validator.expectObject(
724 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
725 |         typeable = false;
726 |         break;
727 |       }
728 | 
729 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
730 |         visitFunction(t, n);
731 |         break;
732 | 
733 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
734 |       case Token.LABEL:
735 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
736 |       case Token.SWITCH:
737 |       case Token.BREAK:
738 |       case Token.CATCH:
739 |       case Token.TRY:
740 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
741 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
742 |       case Token.BLOCK:
743 |       case Token.EMPTY:
744 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
745 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
746 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
747 |       case Token.THROW:
748 |         typeable = false;
749 |         break;
750 | 
751 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
752 |       case Token.DO:
753 |       case Token.FOR:
754 |       case Token.IF:
755 |       case Token.WHILE:
756 |         typeable = false;
757 |         break;
758 | 
759 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
760 |       case Token.AND:
761 |       case Token.HOOK:
762 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
763 |       case Token.OR:
764 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
765 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
766 |         } else {
767 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
768 |           if ((n.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT)
769 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
770 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
771 |           } else {
772 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
773 |           }
774 |         }
775 |         break;
776 | 
777 |       default:
778 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
779 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
780 |         break;
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
784 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
785 | 
786 |     if (typeable) {
787 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
788 |     }
789 | 
790 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
791 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1318-1355
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1318 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1319 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1320 | 
1321 |     FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
1322 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1323 |     if (functionType.isInterface() || functionType.isConstructor()) {
1324 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.
1325 |           getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype().getConstructor();
1326 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1327 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1328 |           (baseConstructor.isConstructor() && functionType.isInterface() ||
1329 |            baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1330 |         compiler.report(
1331 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1332 |       }
1333 | 
1334 |       for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1335 |         boolean badImplementedType = false;
1336 |         ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1337 |         if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1338 |           FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1339 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1340 |           if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1341 |               !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1342 |             badImplementedType = true;
1343 |           }
1344 |         } else {
1345 |           badImplementedType = true;
1346 |         }
1347 |         if (badImplementedType) {
1348 |           report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1349 |         }
1350 |       }
1351 |       if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1352 |         validator.expectAllInterfacePropertiesImplemented(functionType);
1353 |       }
1354 |     }
1355 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)

/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
251 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
252 |     this.type = type;
253 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
254 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
255 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
256 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
257 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
258 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
259 |       int i = 0;
260 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
261 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
262 |         i++;
263 |       }
264 |     }
265 | 
266 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
267 | 
268 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
269 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
270 |       return;
271 |     }
272 | 
273 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
274 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
275 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
276 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
277 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
278 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
279 |       trace.truncateEvents();
280 |     }
281 | 
282 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
283 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
284 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
285 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
286 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
287 |                     + "try/finally block. "
288 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
289 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
290 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     trace.startEvent(this);
294 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getRemainingJSDocLine()

Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the remaining JSDoc line without the {@link JsDocToken#EOL},
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOF} or {@link JsDocToken#EOC}.
   */
Method code:
202 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
203 |   String getRemainingJSDocLine() {
204 |     int c;
205 |     for (;;) {
206 |       c = getChar();
207 |       switch (c) {
208 |         case '*':
209 |           if (peekChar() != '/') {
210 |             addToString(c);
211 |             break;
212 |           }
213 |           // fall through
214 |         case EOF_CHAR:
215 |         case '\n':
216 |           ungetChar(c);
217 |           this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
218 |           stringBufferTop = 0;
219 |           return this.string;
220 | 
221 |         default:
222 |           addToString(c);
223 |           break;
224 |       }
225 |     }
226 |   }

===== M0101 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 236-267
Documentation:
/**
   * If we haven't found a return value yet, try to look at the "return"
   * statements in the function.
   */
Method code:
236 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferReturnStatementsAsLastResort(
237 |       @Nullable Node functionBlock) {
238 |     if (functionBlock == null || compiler.getInput(sourceName).isExtern()) {
239 |       return this;
240 |     }
241 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(functionBlock.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
242 |     if (returnType == null) {
243 |       boolean hasNonEmptyReturns = false;
244 |       List<Node> worklist = Lists.newArrayList(functionBlock);
245 |       while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
246 |         Node current = worklist.remove(worklist.size() - 1);
247 |         int cType = current.getType();
248 |         if (cType == Token.RETURN && current.getFirstChild() != null ||
249 |             cType == Token.THROW) {
250 |           hasNonEmptyReturns = true;
251 |           break;
252 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(current) ||
253 |             NodeUtil.isControlStructure(current)) {
254 |           for (Node child = current.getFirstChild();
255 |                child != null; child = child.getNext()) {
256 |             worklist.add(child);
257 |           }
258 |         }
259 |       }
260 | 
261 |       if (!hasNonEmptyReturns) {
262 |         returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
263 |         returnTypeInferred = true;
264 |       }
265 |     }
266 |     return this;
267 |   }

/**

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.resolve(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)

Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
879 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
880 |     if (resolved) {
881 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
882 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
883 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
884 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
885 |       }
886 |       return resolveResult;
887 |     }
888 |     resolved = true;
889 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
890 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
891 |     return resolveResult;
892 |   }

2115 |   static JSDocInfo getFunctionInfo(Node n) {
2116 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);

2118 |     if (fnInfo == null && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {

===== M0124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 136-794
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
136 |   boolean parse() {
137 |     int lineno;
138 |     int charno;
139 | 
140 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
141 |     JSTypeExpression type;
142 | 
143 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
144 |     JsDocToken token = next();
145 | 
146 |     ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
147 |     token = blockInfo.token;
148 | 
149 |     // If we have a block level comment, record it.
150 |     if (blockInfo.string.length() > 0) {
151 |       jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
152 |     }
153 | 
154 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
155 |     retry: for (;;) {
156 |       switch (token) {
157 |         case ANNOTATION:
158 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
159 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
160 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
161 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
162 | 
163 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
164 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
165 |             if (annotation == null) {
166 |               parser.addWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
167 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
168 |             } else {
169 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
170 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
171 | 
172 |               switch (annotation) {
173 |                 case AUTHOR:
174 |                   ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
175 |                   String author = authorInfo.string;
176 | 
177 |                   if (author.length() == 0) {
178 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
179 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
180 |                   } else {
181 |                     jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
182 |                   }
183 | 
184 |                   token = authorInfo.token;
185 |                   continue retry;
186 | 
187 |                 case CONSTANT:
188 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
189 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
190 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
191 |                   }
192 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
193 |                   continue retry;
194 | 
195 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
196 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
197 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
198 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
199 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
200 |                     } else {
201 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
202 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
203 |                     }
204 |                   }
205 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
206 |                   continue retry;
207 | 
208 |                 case DEPRECATED:
209 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
210 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
211 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
212 |                   }
213 | 
214 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
215 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
216 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
217 | 
218 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
219 | 
220 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
221 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
222 |                   }
223 | 
224 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
225 |                   continue retry;
226 | 
227 |                 case INTERFACE:
228 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
229 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
230 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
231 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
232 |                     } else {
233 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
234 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
235 |                     }
236 |                   }
237 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
238 |                   continue retry;
239 | 
240 |                 case DESC:
241 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
242 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
243 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
244 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
245 |                     continue retry;
246 |                   } else {
247 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
248 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
249 | 
250 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
251 | 
252 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
253 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
254 |                     continue retry;
255 |                   }
256 | 
257 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
258 |                   ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
259 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
260 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
261 | 
262 |                   String fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
263 | 
264 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
265 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
266 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
267 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
268 |                   }
269 |                   token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
270 |                   continue retry;
271 | 
272 |                 case LICENSE:
273 |                 case PRESERVE:
274 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
275 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
276 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
277 | 
278 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
279 | 
280 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
281 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
282 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
283 |                     }
284 |                   }
285 | 
286 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
287 |                   continue retry;
288 | 
289 |                 case ENUM:
290 |                   token = next();
291 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
292 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
293 | 
294 |                   type = null;
295 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
296 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
297 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
298 |                   }
299 | 
300 |                   if (type == null) {
301 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
302 |                   }
303 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
304 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
305 |                   }
306 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
307 |                   continue retry;
308 | 
309 |                 case EXPORT:
310 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
311 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
312 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
313 |                   }
314 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
315 |                   continue retry;
316 | 
317 |                 case EXTERNS:
318 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
319 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
320 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
321 |                   }
322 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
323 |                   continue retry;
324 | 
325 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
326 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
327 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
328 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
329 |                   }
330 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
331 |                   continue retry;
332 | 
333 |                 case EXTENDS:
334 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
335 |                   skipEOLs();
336 |                   token = next();
337 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
338 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
339 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
340 | 
341 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
342 |                     token = next();
343 |                     matchingRc = true;
344 |                   }
345 | 
346 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
347 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
348 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
349 | 
350 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
351 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
352 | 
353 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
354 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
355 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
356 |                     }
357 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
358 | 
359 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
360 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordBaseType(type)) {
361 |                         parser.addWarning(
362 |                             "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
363 |                       }
364 |                     } else {
365 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
366 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
367 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
368 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
369 |                             lineno, charno);
370 |                       }
371 |                     }
372 |                     token = next();
373 |                     if (matchingRc) {
374 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
375 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
376 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
377 |                       }
378 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
379 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
380 |                       parser.addWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
381 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
382 |                     }
383 |                   } else {
384 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
385 |                   }
386 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
387 |                   continue retry;
388 | 
389 |                 case HIDDEN:
390 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
391 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
392 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
393 |                   }
394 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
395 |                   continue retry;
396 | 
397 |                 case NO_ALIAS:
398 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoAlias()) {
399 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noalias",
400 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
401 |                   }
402 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
403 |                   continue retry;
404 | 
405 |                 case NO_COMPILE:
406 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoCompile()) {
407 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocompile",
408 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
409 |                   }
410 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
411 |                   continue retry;
412 | 
413 |                 case NO_TYPE_CHECK:
414 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoTypeCheck()) {
415 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nocheck",
416 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
417 |                   }
418 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
419 |                   continue retry;
420 | 
421 |                 case NOT_IMPLEMENTED:
422 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
423 |                   continue retry;
424 | 
425 |                 case INHERIT_DOC:
426 |                 case OVERRIDE:
427 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordOverride()) {
428 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.override",
429 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
430 |                   }
431 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
432 |                   continue retry;
433 | 
434 |                 case THROWS:
435 |                   skipEOLs();
436 |                   token = next();
437 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
438 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
439 |                   type = null;
440 | 
441 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
442 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
443 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
444 | 
445 |                     if (type == null) {
446 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
447 |                       // recovering parsing
448 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
449 |                       continue retry;
450 |                     }
451 |                   }
452 | 
453 |                   // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
454 |                   token = current();
455 | 
456 |                   // Save the throw type.
457 |                   jsdocBuilder.recordThrowType(type);
458 | 
459 |                   // Find the throw's description (if applicable).
460 |                   ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
461 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
462 | 
463 |                   String description = descriptionInfo.string;
464 | 
465 |                   if (description.length() > 0) {
466 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordThrowDescription(type, description);
467 |                   }
468 | 
469 |                   token = descriptionInfo.token;
470 |                   continue retry;
471 | 
472 |                 case PARAM:
473 |                   skipEOLs();
474 |                   token = next();
475 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
476 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
477 |                   type = null;
478 | 
479 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
480 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
481 |                         parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(token));
482 | 
483 |                     if (type == null) {
484 |                       // parsing error reported during recursive descent
485 |                       // recovering parsing
486 |                       token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
487 |                       continue retry;
488 |                     }
489 |                     skipEOLs();
490 |                     token = next();
491 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
492 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
493 |                   }
494 | 
495 |                   String name = null;
496 |                   boolean isBracketedParam = JsDocToken.LB == token;
497 |                   if (isBracketedParam) {
498 |                     token = next();
499 |                   }
500 | 
501 |                   if (JsDocToken.STRING != token) {
502 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.missing.variable.name",
503 |                         lineno, charno);
504 |                   } else {
505 |                     name = stream.getString();
506 | 
507 |                     if (isBracketedParam) {
508 |                       token = next();
509 | 
510 |                       // Throw out JsDocToolkit's "default" parameter annotation.
511 |                       // It makes no sense under our type system.
512 |                       if (JsDocToken.EQUALS == token) {
513 |                         token = next();
514 |                         if (JsDocToken.STRING == token) {
515 |                           token = next();
516 |                         }
517 |                       }
518 | 
519 |                       if (JsDocToken.RB != token) {
520 |                         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rb");
521 |                       } else if (type != null) {
522 |                         // Make the type expression optional, if it isn't
523 |                         // already.
524 |                         type = JSTypeExpression.makeOptionalArg(type);
525 |                       }
526 |                     }
527 | 
528 |                     // If the param name has a DOT in it, just throw it out
529 |                     // quietly. We do not handle the JsDocToolkit method
530 |                     // for handling properties of params.
531 |                     if (name.indexOf('.') > -1) {
532 |                       name = null;
533 |                     } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordParameter(name, type)) {
534 |                       if (jsdocBuilder.hasParameter(name)) {
535 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.dup.variable.name", name,
536 |                             lineno, charno);
537 |                       } else {
538 |                         parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", name,
539 |                             lineno, charno);
540 |                       }
541 |                     }
542 |                   }
543 | 
544 |                   if (name == null) {
545 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
546 |                     continue retry;
547 |                   }
548 | 
549 |                   jsdocBuilder.markName(name, lineno, charno);
550 | 
551 |                   // Find the parameter's description (if applicable).
552 |                   ExtractionInfo paramDescriptionInfo =
553 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
554 | 
555 |                   String paramDescription = paramDescriptionInfo.string;
556 | 
557 |                   if (paramDescription.length() > 0) {
558 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordParameterDescription(name,
559 |                                                             paramDescription);
560 |                   }
561 | 
562 |                   token = paramDescriptionInfo.token;
563 |                   continue retry;
564 | 
565 |                 case PRESERVE_TRY:
566 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordPreserveTry()) {
567 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.preservertry",
568 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
569 |                   }
570 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
571 |                   continue retry;
572 | 
573 |                 case PRIVATE:
574 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PRIVATE)) {
575 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.private",
576 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
577 |                   }
578 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
579 |                   continue retry;
580 | 
581 |                 case PROTECTED:
582 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PROTECTED)) {
583 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.protected",
584 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
585 |                   }
586 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
587 |                   continue retry;
588 | 
589 |                 case PUBLIC:
590 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVisibility(Visibility.PUBLIC)) {
591 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.visibility.public",
592 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
593 |                   }
594 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
595 |                   continue retry;
596 | 
597 |                 case NO_SHADOW:
598 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoShadow()) {
599 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.noshadow",
600 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
601 |                   }
602 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
603 |                   continue retry;
604 | 
605 |                 case NO_SIDE_EFFECTS:
606 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordNoSideEffects()) {
607 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.nosideeffects",
608 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
609 |                   }
610 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
611 |                   continue retry;
612 | 
613 |                 case IMPLICIT_CAST:
614 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplicitCast()) {
615 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.implicitcast",
616 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
617 |                   }
618 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
619 |                   continue retry;
620 | 
621 |                 case SEE:
622 |                   ExtractionInfo referenceInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
623 |                   String reference = referenceInfo.string;
624 | 
625 |                   if (reference.length() == 0) {
626 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.seemissing",
627 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
628 |                   } else {
629 |                     jsdocBuilder.addReference(reference);
630 |                   }
631 | 
632 |                   token = referenceInfo.token;
633 |                   continue retry;
634 | 
635 |                 case SUPPRESS:
636 |                   token = parseSuppressTag(next());
637 |                   continue retry;
638 | 
639 |                 case TEMPLATE:
640 |                   ExtractionInfo templateInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
641 |                   String templateTypeName = templateInfo.string;
642 | 
643 |                   if (templateTypeName.length() == 0) {
644 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.templatemissing",
645 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
646 |                   } else if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTemplateTypeName(
647 |                       templateTypeName)) {
648 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.template.at.most.once",
649 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
650 |                   }
651 | 
652 |                   token = templateInfo.token;
653 |                   continue retry;
654 | 
655 |                 case VERSION:
656 |                   ExtractionInfo versionInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
657 |                   String version = versionInfo.string;
658 | 
659 |                   if (version.length() == 0) {
660 |                     parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.versionmissing",
661 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
662 |                   } else {
663 |                     if (!jsdocBuilder.recordVersion(version)) {
664 |                        parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.extraversion",
665 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
666 |                     }
667 |                   }
668 | 
669 |                   token = versionInfo.token;
670 |                   continue retry;
671 | 
672 |                 case DEFINE:
673 |                 case RETURN:
674 |                 case THIS:
675 |                 case TYPE:
676 |                 case TYPEDEF:
677 |                   skipEOLs();
678 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
679 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
680 | 
681 |                   token = next();
682 | 
683 |                   Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNode(token, lineno, charno);
684 | 
685 |                   if (annotation == Annotation.THIS) {
686 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
687 |                     if (typeNode != null && token != JsDocToken.LC) {
688 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
689 |                     }
690 |                   }
691 |                   type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
692 | 
693 |                   if (type == null) {
694 |                     // error reported during recursive descent
695 |                     // recovering parsing
696 |                   } else {
697 |                     switch (annotation) {
698 |                       case DEFINE:
699 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDefineType(type)) {
700 |                           parser.addWarning("msg.jsdoc.define",
701 |                               lineno, charno);
702 |                         }
703 |                         break;
704 | 
705 |                       case RETURN:
706 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordReturnType(type)) {
707 |                           parser.addWarning(
708 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
709 |                           break;
710 |                         }
711 | 
712 |                         // *Update* the token to that after the type annotation.
713 |                         token = current();
714 | 
715 |                         // Find the return's description (if applicable).
716 |                         ExtractionInfo returnDescriptionInfo =
717 |                             extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
718 | 
719 |                         String returnDescription = returnDescriptionInfo.string;
720 | 
721 |                         if (returnDescription.length() > 0) {
722 |                           jsdocBuilder.recordReturnDescription(
723 |                               returnDescription);
724 |                         }
725 | 
726 |                         token = returnDescriptionInfo.token;
727 |                         continue retry;
728 | 
729 |                       case THIS:
730 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordThisType(type)) {
731 |                           parser.addWarning(
732 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
733 |                         }
734 |                         break;
735 | 
736 |                       case TYPE:
737 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
738 |                           parser.addWarning(
739 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
740 |                         }
741 |                         break;
742 | 
743 |                       case TYPEDEF:
744 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordTypedef(type)) {
745 |                           parser.addWarning(
746 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
747 |                         }
748 |                         break;
749 |                     }
750 | 
751 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
752 |                   }
753 |                   continue retry;
754 |               }
755 |             }
756 |           }
757 |           break;
758 | 
759 |         case EOC:
760 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
761 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
762 |           }
763 | 
764 |           return true;
765 | 
766 |         case EOF:
767 |           // discard any accumulated information
768 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);
769 |           parser.addWarning("msg.unexpected.eof",
770 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
771 |           return false;
772 | 
773 |         case EOL:
774 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
775 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
776 |           }
777 |           token = next();
778 |           continue retry;
779 | 
780 |         default:
781 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
782 |             token = next();
783 |             continue retry;
784 |           } else {
785 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
786 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
787 |             continue retry;
788 |           }
789 |       }
790 | 
791 |       // next token
792 |       token = next();
793 |     }
794 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 192-211
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
192 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
193 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
194 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
195 |     worklist.add(entry);
196 | 
197 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
198 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
199 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
200 |         continue;
201 |       }
202 | 
203 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
204 | 
205 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
206 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
207 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
208 |         worklist.add(candidate);
209 |       }
210 |     }
211 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1100-1129
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1100 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1101 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1102 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1103 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1104 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1105 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1106 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1107 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1108 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1109 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1110 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1111 |         parentNodeType == Token.LP ||
1112 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1113 |       return false;
1114 |     }
1115 | 
1116 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1117 |     if (type == null) {
1118 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1119 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1120 |       if (var != null) {
1121 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1122 |         if (varType != null) {
1123 |           type = varType;
1124 |         }
1125 |       }
1126 |     }
1127 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1128 |     return true;
1129 |   }

===== M0148 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 684-720
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
684 |   @Override
685 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
686 |     if (this.isEquivalentTo(that)) {
687 |       return true;
688 |     }
689 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
690 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
691 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
692 |         return true;
693 |       }
694 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
695 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
696 |         return false;
697 |       }
698 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
699 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
700 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
701 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
702 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
703 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
704 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
705 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
706 |       return (this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
707 |               other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
708 |               this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis)) &&
709 |           this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
710 |     }
711 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
712 |       UnionType union = (UnionType) that;
713 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
714 |         if (this.isSubtype(element)) {
715 |           return true;
716 |         }
717 |       }
718 |     }
719 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
720 |   }
