===== M0152 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1399-1430
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1399 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1400 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1401 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1402 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1403 | 
1404 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1405 |     int ordinal = 0;
1406 |     Node parameter = null;
1407 |     Node argument = null;
1408 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1409 |            parameters.hasNext()) {
1410 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1411 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1412 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1413 |       argument = arguments.next();
1414 |       ordinal++;
1415 | 
1416 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1417 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1418 |     }
1419 | 
1420 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1421 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1422 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1423 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1424 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1425 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1426 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1427 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1428 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1429 |     }
1430 |   }

===== M0158 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 202-230
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
202 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
203 |     initialize();
204 |     int step = 0;
205 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
206 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
207 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
208 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
209 |       }
210 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
211 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
212 |       joinInputs(curNode);
213 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
214 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
215 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
216 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
217 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
218 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
219 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
220 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
221 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
222 |           }
223 |         }
224 |       }
225 |       step++;
226 |     }
227 |     if (isForward()) {
228 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
229 |     }
230 |   }

===== M0163 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 605-641
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
605 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
606 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
607 |         fnName, sourceNode, parametersNode, returnType);
608 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
609 | 
610 |     if (existingType != null) {
611 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType instanceof InstanceObjectType;
612 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
613 |         FunctionType existingFn =
614 |             isInstanceObject ?
615 |             ((InstanceObjectType) existingType).getConstructor() :
616 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
617 | 
618 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
619 |           existingFn.setSource(sourceNode);
620 |         }
621 | 
622 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
623 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
624 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
625 |         }
626 | 
627 |         return existingFn;
628 |       } else {
629 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
630 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
631 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
632 |       }
633 |     }
634 | 
635 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
636 | 
637 |     if (scope.isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
638 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
639 |     }
640 |     return fnType;
641 |   }

===== M0186 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 586-612
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
586 |   @Override
587 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
588 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
589 |       return false;
590 |     }
591 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
592 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
593 |       return false;
594 |     }
595 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
596 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
597 |         return this == that;
598 |       }
599 |       return false;
600 |     }
601 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
602 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
603 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
604 |       }
605 |       return false;
606 |     }
607 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
608 |       return false;
609 |     }
610 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
611 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
612 |   }

Original range: 56-67
Documentation:

Method code:
56 |   void addGuard(WarningsGuard guard) {
57 |     if (guard instanceof ComposeWarningsGuard) {
58 |       // Reverse the guards, so that they have the same order in the result.
59 |       addGuards(Iterables.reverse(((ComposeWarningsGuard) guard).guards));
60 |     } else {
61 |       int index = Collections.binarySearch(this.guards, guard, guardComparator);
62 |       if (index < 0) {
63 |         index = -index - 1;
64 |       }
65 |       this.guards.add(index, guard);
66 |     }
67 |   }

===== M0199 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 60-196
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
60 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
61 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
62 |     int c;
63 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
64 |     for (;;) {
65 |       // eat white spaces
66 |       for (;;) {
67 |         charno = -1;
68 |         c = getChar();
69 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
70 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
71 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
72 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
73 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
74 |           break;
75 |         }
76 |       }
77 | 
78 |       switch (c) {
79 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
80 |         case '@':
81 |           do {
82 |             c = getChar();
83 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
84 |               addToString(c);
85 |             } else {
86 |               ungetChar(c);
87 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
88 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
89 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
90 |             }
91 |           } while (true);
92 | 
93 |         case '*':
94 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
95 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
96 |           } else {
97 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
98 |           }
99 | 
100 |         case ',':
101 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
102 | 
103 |         case '>':
104 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
105 | 
106 |         case '(':
107 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
108 | 
109 |         case ')':
110 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
111 | 
112 |         case '{':
113 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
114 | 
115 |         case '}':
116 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
117 | 
118 |         case '[':
119 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
120 | 
121 |         case ']':
122 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
123 | 
124 |         case '?':
125 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
126 | 
127 |         case '!':
128 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
129 | 
130 |         case ':':
131 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
132 | 
133 |         case '=':
134 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
135 | 
136 |         case '|':
137 |           matchChar('|');
138 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
139 | 
140 |         case '.':
141 |           c = getChar();
142 |           if (c == '<') {
143 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
144 |           } else {
145 |             if (c == '.') {
146 |               c = getChar();
147 |               if (c == '.') {
148 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
149 |               } else {
150 |                 addToString('.');
151 |               }
152 |             }
153 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
154 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
155 |             c = '.';
156 |           }
157 |           // fall through
158 | 
159 |         default: {
160 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
161 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
162 |           int c1 = c;
163 |           addToString(c);
164 |           int c2 = getChar();
165 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
166 |             ungetChar(c2);
167 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
168 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
169 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
170 |           } else {
171 |             do {
172 |               c1 = c2;
173 |               c2 = getChar();
174 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
175 |                 ungetChar(c2);
176 |                 ungetChar(c1);
177 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
178 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
179 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
180 |               } else {
181 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
182 |                   addToString(c1);
183 |                 } else {
184 |                   ungetChar(c2);
185 |                   addToString(c1);
186 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
187 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
188 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
189 |                 }
190 |               }
191 |             } while (true);
192 |           }
193 |         }
194 |       }
195 |     }
196 |   }

===== M0200 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 588-614
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
588 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
589 |     ObjectType owner = null;
590 | 
591 |     // Properties can only be defined on object types, so normalize everything
592 |     // to discrete object types.
593 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType) {
594 |       owner = (ObjectType) type;
595 |     } else if (getNativeType(ALL_TYPE).isSubtype(type)) {
596 |       owner = getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE);
597 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
598 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
599 |         registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, alternate);
600 |       }
601 |     }
602 | 
603 |     if (owner == null) {
604 |       return;
605 |     }
606 | 
607 |     Set<ObjectType> typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
608 |     if (typeSet == null) {
609 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(
610 |           propertyName, typeSet = new LinkedHashSet<ObjectType>());
611 |     }
612 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
613 |     typeSet.add(owner);
614 |   }

161 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
162 |     // Verify the nodes.

===== M0205 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParametersOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 850-872
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
850 |   private void updateTypeOfParametersOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
851 |     int i = 0;
852 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
853 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
854 |       JSType iParameterType = iParameter.getJSType();
855 |       if (iParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
856 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = (FunctionType) iParameterType;
857 | 
858 |         if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
859 |           // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
860 |           return;
861 |         }
862 |         Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
863 |         JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
864 |         if (iArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
865 |             iArgumentType instanceof FunctionType &&
866 |             iArgumentType.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
867 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
868 |         }
869 |       }
870 |       i++;
871 |     }
872 |   }

/**
   * Determines whether a node represents an object literal key
   * (e.g. key1 in {key1: value1, key2: value2}).
   *
   * @param node A node
   * @param parent The node's parent
   */
Method code:
1420 |   static boolean isObjectLitKey(Node node, Node parent) {
1421 |     if (node.getType() == Token.STRING && parent.getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) {
1422 |       int index = 0;
1423 |       for (Node current = parent.getFirstChild();
1424 |            current != null;
1425 |            current = current.getNext()) {
1426 |         if (current == node) {
1427 |           return index % 2 == 0;
1428 |         }
1429 |         index++;
1430 |       }
1431 |     }
1432 |     return false;
1433 |   }

===== M0218 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 82-178
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
83 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
84 | 
85 |     switch (n.getType()) {
86 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
87 |       case Token.NAME:
88 |         if (parent == null) {
89 |           return;
90 |         }
91 | 
92 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
93 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
94 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
95 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
96 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
97 |           return;
98 |         }
99 | 
100 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
101 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
102 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
103 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
104 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
105 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
106 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
107 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
108 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
109 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
110 |         if (docInfo == null &&
111 |             !(parent.getType() == Token.VAR &&
112 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
113 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
114 |         }
115 | 
116 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
117 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
118 |         if (varType == null && parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
119 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
120 |         }
121 | 
122 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
123 |         // we can do.
124 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
125 |           return;
126 |         }
127 | 
128 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
129 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
130 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
131 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
132 |           return;
133 |         }
134 | 
135 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
136 |         break;
137 | 
138 |       case Token.GETPROP:
139 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
140 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
141 |         //
142 |         // 1)
143 |         // /** @deprecated */
144 |         // obj.prop = ...
145 |         //
146 |         // 2)
147 |         // /** @deprecated */
148 |         // obj.prop;
149 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
150 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN &&
151 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
152 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
153 |           if (docInfo == null) {
154 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
155 |           }
156 |           if (docInfo != null) {
157 |             ObjectType lhsType =
158 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
159 |             if (lhsType != null) {
160 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
161 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
162 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
163 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo, inExterns);
164 |               }
165 | 
166 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
167 |               ObjectType propType =
168 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
169 |               if (propType != null) {
170 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
171 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
172 |               }
173 |             }
174 |           }
175 |         }
176 |         break;
177 |     }
178 |   }

459 |     protected void joinInputs(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {

461 |       List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> predNodes =
462 |           getCfg().getDirectedPredNodes(node);
463 |       List<L> values = new ArrayList<L>(predNodes.size());

467 |

469 |             getCfg().getDirectedSuccNodes(predNode).indexOf(node));
470 |

472 |       }
473 |       if (getCfg().getEntry() == node) {
474 |         state.setIn(createEntryLattice());
475 |       } else if (!values.isEmpty()) {
476 |         state.setIn(joinOp.apply(values));
477 |       }

===== M0232 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 800-844
Documentation:

Method code:
800 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
801 |       Node callNode) {
802 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
803 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
804 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
805 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
806 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
807 |       return scope;
808 |     }
809 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
810 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
811 |       return scope;
812 |     }
813 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
814 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
815 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
816 |     if (assertedType == null) {
817 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
818 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
819 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
820 |         if (type != narrowed) {
821 |           scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
822 |           redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
823 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
824 |         }
825 |       } else if (assertedNode.getType() == Token.AND ||
826 |                  assertedNode.getType() == Token.OR) {
827 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
828 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
829 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
830 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
831 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
832 |       }
833 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
834 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
835 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
836 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
837 |       if (type != narrowed) {
838 |         scope = scope.createChildFlowScope();
839 |         redeclare(scope, assertedNodeName, narrowed);
840 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
841 |       }
842 |     }
843 |     return scope;
844 |   }

===== M0240 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 496-542
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
496 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
497 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
498 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
499 |     switch (left.getType()) {
500 |       case Token.NAME:
501 |         String varName = left.getString();
502 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
503 |         if (var != null && var.isLocal() && var.getScope() != syntacticScope) {
504 |           assignedOuterLocalVars.put(var.getScope(), var);
505 |         }
506 | 
507 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
508 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
509 |         // This has two purposes:
510 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
511 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
512 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
513 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
514 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
515 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
516 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
517 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
518 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
519 |           redeclare(scope, varName, resultType);
520 |         }
521 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
522 |             resultType : null);
523 | 
524 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
525 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
526 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
527 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
528 |         }
529 |         break;
530 |       case Token.GETPROP:
531 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
532 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
533 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(qualifiedName,
534 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
535 |               resultType);
536 |         }
537 | 
538 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
539 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
540 |         break;
541 |     }
542 |   }

===== M0243 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 119-140
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
119 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
120 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
121 |     String methodName = null;
122 |     if (callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
123 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
124 |     } else if (callName.getType() == Token.NAME) {
125 |       String name = callName.getString();
126 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
127 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
128 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
129 |       }
130 |     }
131 | 
132 |     if (methodName != null) {
133 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
134 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
135 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
136 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
137 |       }
138 |     }
139 |     return null;
140 |   }

/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1394 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1395 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);

1397 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1398 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1399 |         next();

1402 |           next();
1403 |         }
1404 |         skipEOLs();
1405 |         token = next();

1407 |       }
1408 |     }

===== M0249 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 383-398
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
383 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
384 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
385 |       Set<String> directives = null;
386 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
387 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
388 |         if (directives == null) {
389 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
390 |         } else {
391 |           directives.add(directive);
392 |         }
393 |       }
394 | 
395 |       if (directives != null) {
396 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
397 |       }
398 |     }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 750-815
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a typed variable. The defining node will be annotated with the
     * variable's type of {@link JSTypeNative#UNKNOWN_TYPE} if its type is
     * inferred.
     *
     * Slots may be any variable or any qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     */
Method code:
750 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, JSType type, boolean inferred) {
751 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(inferred || type != null);
752 | 
753 |       // Only allow declarations of NAMEs and qualfied names.
754 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = false;
755 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
756 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
757 |             parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
758 |             parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
759 |             parent.getType() == Token.LP ||
760 |             parent.getType() == Token.CATCH);
761 |         shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis = scope.isGlobal() &&
762 |             (parent.getType() == Token.VAR ||
763 |              parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
764 |       } else {
765 |         Preconditions.checkArgument(
766 |             n.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
767 |             (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN ||
768 |              parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT));
769 |       }
770 |       String variableName = n.getQualifiedName();
771 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
772 | 
773 |       // declared in closest scope?
774 |       if (scope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
775 |         Var oldVar = scope.getVar(variableName);
776 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
777 |             sourceName, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
778 |       } else {
779 |         if (!inferred) {
780 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
781 |         }
782 |         CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(sourceName);
783 |         scope.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
784 | 
785 |         if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
786 |           ObjectType globalThis =
787 |               typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
788 |           boolean isExtern = input.isExtern();
789 |           if (inferred) {
790 |             globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
791 |                 type == null ?
792 |                     typeRegistry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
793 |                     type,
794 |                 isExtern);
795 |           } else {
796 |             globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, isExtern);
797 |           }
798 |         }
799 | 
800 |         // If we're in the global scope, also declare var.prototype
801 |         // in the scope chain.
802 |         if (scope.isGlobal() && type instanceof FunctionType) {
803 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
804 |           if (fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) {
805 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
806 |             scope.declare(variableName + ".prototype", n,
807 |                 fnType.getPrototype(), compiler.getInput(sourceName),
808 |                 /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
809 |                 superClassCtor == null ||
810 |                 superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
811 |                     typeRegistry.getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
812 |           }
813 |         }
814 |       }
815 |     }

===== M0254 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 530-580
Documentation:

Method code:
530 |    @Override
531 |   Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
532 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
533 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
534 |       if (name == null) {
535 |         name = new Name();
536 |         name.setIdentifier("");
537 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
538 |       }
539 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
540 |       Node newName = transform(name);
541 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
542 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
543 |         // declaration.
544 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
545 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
546 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
547 |         // same line as the function keyword.
548 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
549 |             functionNode.getLp();
550 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
551 |       }
552 | 
553 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
554 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
555 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
556 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
557 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
558 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
559 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
560 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
561 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
562 |       if (fnName != null) {
563 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
564 |       } else {
565 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
566 |       }
567 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
568 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
569 | 
570 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
571 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
572 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
573 |       }
574 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
575 | 
576 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
577 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
578 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
579 |      return node;
580 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.GlobalScopeBuilder.checkForTypedef(NodeTraversal,Node,JSDocInfo)

/**
     * Handle typedefs.
     * @param t The current traversal.
     * @param candidate A qualified name node.
     * @param info JSDoc comments.
     */
Method code:
1024 |     private void checkForTypedef(
1025 |         NodeTraversal t, Node candidate, JSDocInfo info) {
1026 |       if (info == null || !info.hasTypedefType()) {
1027 |         return;
1028 |       }
1029 | 
1030 |       String typedef = candidate.getQualifiedName();
1031 |       if (typedef == null) {
1032 |         return;
1033 |       }
1034 | 
1035 |       // TODO(nicksantos|user): This is a terrible, terrible hack
1036 |       // to bail out on recusive typedefs. We'll eventually need
1037 |       // to handle these properly.
1038 |       typeRegistry.forwardDeclareType(typedef);
1039 | 
1040 |       JSType realType = info.getTypedefType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
1041 |       if (realType == null) {
1042 |         compiler.report(
1043 |             JSError.make(
1044 |                 t.getSourceName(), candidate, MALFORMED_TYPEDEF, typedef));
1045 |       }
1046 | 
1047 |       typeRegistry.declareType(typedef, realType);
1048 |       if (candidate.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
1049 |         defineSlot(candidate, candidate.getParent(),
1050 |             typeRegistry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE), false);
1051 |       }
1052 |     }

===== M0259 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.GlobalScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1242-1272
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1242 |     private void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1243 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1244 |         Node n = stub.node;
1245 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1246 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1247 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1248 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1249 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1250 | 
1251 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1252 |           continue;
1253 |         }
1254 | 
1255 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1256 |         // in the type registry.
1257 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1258 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1259 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1260 | 
1261 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1262 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1263 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1264 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1265 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1266 |               propName, unknownType, isExtern);
1267 |         } else {
1268 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1269 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1270 |         }
1271 |       }
1272 |     }

===== M0260 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 816-843
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
816 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
817 |     // unknown
818 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
819 |       return true;
820 |     }
821 |     // equality
822 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
823 |       return true;
824 |     }
825 |     // all type
826 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
827 |       return true;
828 |     }
829 |     // unions
830 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
831 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
832 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
833 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
834 |           return true;
835 |         }
836 |       }
837 |     }
838 |     // named types
839 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
840 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).referencedType);
841 |     }
842 |     return false;
843 |   }

===== M0266 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 61-78
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
61 |   @Override
62 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
63 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
64 |       return false;
65 |     }
66 | 
67 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
68 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
69 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
70 |     if (pos >= 0) {
71 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
72 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
73 |         return false;
74 |       }
75 |     }
76 | 
77 |     return isConstantKey(name);
78 |   }

===== M0267 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 71-112
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
71 |   @Override
72 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
73 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
74 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
75 |     if (type != null) {
76 |       Node subclass = null;
77 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
78 | 
79 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
80 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
81 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
82 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
83 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
84 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
85 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
86 |       if (callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
87 |           callName.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
88 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
89 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
90 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
91 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
92 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
93 |       }
94 | 
95 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
96 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
97 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
98 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
99 |       if (subclass != null &&
100 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
101 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
102 |         // make sure to strip the prototype off of the nodes
103 |         // to normalize for goog.mixin
104 |         return new SubclassRelationship(
105 |             type,
106 |             stripPrototype(subclass),
107 |             stripPrototype(superclass));
108 |       }
109 |     }
110 | 
111 |     return null;
112 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionType.resolveInternal(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)

Method code:
505 |   @Override
506 |   JSType resolveInternal(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
507 |     setResolvedTypeInternal(this); // for circularly defined types.
508 | 
509 |     boolean changed = false;
510 |     ImmutableList.Builder<JSType> resolvedTypes = ImmutableList.builder();
511 |     for (JSType alternate : alternates) {
512 |       JSType newAlternate = alternate.resolve(t, scope);
513 |       changed |= (alternate != newAlternate);
514 |       resolvedTypes.add(alternate);
515 |     }
516 |     if (changed) {
517 |       Collection<JSType> newAlternates = resolvedTypes.build();
518 |       Preconditions.checkState(
519 |           newAlternates.hashCode() == this.hashcode);
520 |       alternates = newAlternates;
521 |     }
522 |     return this;
523 |   }

===== M0274 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 809-836
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
809 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
810 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
811 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
812 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
813 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunction(handler)) {
814 |           return;
815 |         }
816 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.getType() == Token.TRY);
817 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
818 | 
819 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
820 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
821 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
822 |           } else {
823 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
824 |           }
825 |         } else { // Has a catch.
826 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
827 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
828 |             return;
829 |           } else {
830 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
831 |           }
832 |         }
833 |         lastJump = handler;
834 |       }
835 |     }
836 |   }

===== M0275 =====

===== M0286 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 184-283
Documentation:

Method code:
184 |   @Override
185 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
186 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
187 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
188 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
189 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
190 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
191 |     // worthwhile.
192 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
193 |     Node condition = null;
194 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
195 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
196 | 
197 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
198 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
199 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
200 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
201 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
202 | 
203 |       switch (branch) {
204 |         case ON_TRUE:
205 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
206 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
207 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
208 |             if (item.getType() == Token.VAR) {
209 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
210 |             }
211 |             if (item.getType() == Token.NAME) {
212 |               FlowScope informed = output.createChildFlowScope();
213 |               redeclare(informed, item.getString(),
214 |                   getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
215 |               newScope = informed;
216 |             }
217 |             break;
218 |           }
219 | 
220 |           // FALL THROUGH
221 | 
222 |         case ON_FALSE:
223 |           if (condition == null) {
224 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
225 |             if (condition == null && source.getType() == Token.CASE) {
226 |               condition = source;
227 | 
228 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
229 |               // of the loop.
230 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
231 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
232 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
233 |               }
234 |             }
235 |           }
236 | 
237 |           if (condition != null) {
238 |             if (condition.getType() == Token.AND ||
239 |                 condition.getType() == Token.OR) {
240 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
241 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
242 |               // scope on false.
243 |               //
244 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
245 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
246 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
247 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
248 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
249 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
250 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
251 | 
252 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
253 |               // of the loop.
254 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
255 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.getType() == Token.AND ?
256 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
257 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
258 |               }
259 |               newScope =
260 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
261 |                       condition,
262 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
263 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
264 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
265 |             } else {
266 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
267 |               // of the loop.
268 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
269 |                 conditionFlowScope =
270 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
271 |               }
272 |               newScope =
273 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
274 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
275 |             }
276 |           }
277 |           break;
278 |       }
279 | 
280 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
281 |     }
282 |     return result;
283 |   }

===== M0296 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 87-142
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
87 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
88 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
89 |     // just bail out early here.
90 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
91 |       return this;
92 |     }
93 | 
94 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
95 | 
96 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
97 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
98 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
99 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
100 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
101 |     }
102 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
103 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
104 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
105 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
106 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
107 |         }
108 |       } else {
109 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
110 |           return this;
111 |         }
112 | 
113 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
114 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
115 |         // one another.
116 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
117 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
118 |           JSType current = it.next();
119 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
120 |               current.isUnknownType()) {
121 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
122 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
123 |               return this;
124 |             }
125 |           } else {
126 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
127 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
128 |               return this;
129 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
130 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
131 |               it.remove();
132 |             }
133 |           }
134 |         }
135 |         alternates.add(alternate);
136 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
137 |       }
138 |     } else {
139 |       result = null;
140 |     }
141 |     return this;
142 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.JsAst.parse(AbstractCompiler,String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/JsAst.java
Original range: 85-107

Method code:
85 |   private void parse(AbstractCompiler compiler, String sourceName,
86 |       String sourceStr) {
87 |     try {
88 |       logger_.fine("Parsing: " + sourceName);
89 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,
90 |           compiler.getParserConfig(),
91 |           compiler.getDefaultErrorReporter(),
92 |           logger_);
93 |     } catch (IOException e) {
94 |       compiler.report(JSError.make(AbstractCompiler.READ_ERROR, sourceName));
95 |     }
96 | 
97 |     if (root == null || compiler.hasHaltingErrors()) {
98 |       // There was a parse error or IOException, so use a dummy block.
99 |       root = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
100 |     } else {
101 |       compiler.prepareAst(root);
102 |     }
103 | 
104 |     // Set the source name so that the compiler passes can track
105 |     // the source file and module.
106 |     root.putProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP, sourceName);
107 |   }

===== M0298 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 213-288
Documentation:

Method code:
213 |   @Override
214 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
215 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
216 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
217 | 
218 |     switch (n.getType()) {
219 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
220 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
221 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
222 |           return true;
223 |         }
224 |         return false;
225 |       case Token.TRY:
226 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
227 |         return true;
228 |     }
229 | 
230 |     /*
231 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
232 |      * is.
233 |      *
234 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
235 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
236 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
237 |      *
238 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
239 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
240 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
241 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
242 |      *
243 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
244 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
245 |      */
246 |     if (parent != null) {
247 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
248 |         case Token.FOR:
249 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
250 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
251 | 
252 |         // Skip the conditions.
253 |         case Token.IF:
254 |         case Token.WHILE:
255 |         case Token.WITH:
256 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
257 |         case Token.DO:
258 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
259 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
260 |         case Token.SWITCH:
261 |         case Token.CASE:
262 |         case Token.CATCH:
263 |         case Token.LABEL:
264 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
265 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
266 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
267 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
268 |         case Token.BREAK:
269 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
270 |         case Token.VAR:
271 |         case Token.RETURN:
272 |         case Token.THROW:
273 |           return false;
274 |         case Token.TRY:
275 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
276 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
277 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
278 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
279 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
280 |            */
281 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
282 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
283 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
284 |           }
285 |       }
286 |     }
287 |     return true;
288 |   }

/**
   * Checks that the prototype is an implicit prototype of this object. Since
   * each object has an implicit prototype, an implicit prototype's
   * implicit prototype is also this implicit prototype's.
   *
   * @param prototype any prototype based object
   *
   * @return {@code true} if {@code prototype} is {@code equal} to any

384 |       if (current.isEquivalentTo(prototype)) {

   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
396 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
397 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
398 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
399 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
400 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {
401 |       for (CompilerInput input : module.getInputs()) {
402 |         String inputName = input.getName();
403 | 
404 |         // NOTE(nicksantos): If an input is in more than one module,
405 |         // it will show up twice in the inputs list, and then we
406 |         // will get an error down the line.
407 |         inputs.add(input);
408 |         inputMap.put(inputName, module);
409 |       }
410 |     }
411 |     return inputs;
412 |   }

===== M0315 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 394-438
Documentation:

Method code:
394 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
395 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
396 |       cursor++;
397 |       --ungetCursor;
398 |       if (charno == -1) {
399 |         charno = getOffset();
400 |       }
401 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
402 |     }
403 | 
404 |     for(;;) {
405 |       int c;
406 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
407 |         hitEOF = true;
408 |         if (charno == -1) {
409 |           charno = getOffset();
410 |         }
411 |         return EOF_CHAR;
412 |       }
413 |       cursor++;
414 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
415 | 
416 | 
417 |       if (c <= 127) {
418 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
419 |           lineEndChar = c;
420 |           c = '\n';
421 |         }
422 |       } else {
423 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
424 |           continue;
425 |         }
426 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
427 |           lineEndChar = c;
428 |           c = '\n';
429 |         }
430 |       }
431 | 
432 |       if (charno == -1) {
433 |         charno = getOffset();
434 |       }
435 | 
436 |       return c;
437 |     }
438 |   }

965 |     /**
966 |      * Returns a node comparator based on the pre-order traversal of the AST.
967 |      * @param isForward x 'before' y in the pre-order traversal implies
968 |      * x 'less than' y (if true) and x 'greater than' y (if false).

982 |           @Override

984 |               DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n1, DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> n2) {

===== M0319 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitGetProp(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1139-1165
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1139 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1140 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1141 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1142 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1143 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN) {
1144 |       return;
1145 |     }
1146 | 
1147 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1148 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1149 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1150 |     // and what isn't.
1151 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1152 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1153 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1154 | 
1155 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1156 |     // non-object.
1157 |     if (!validator.expectNotVoid(t, n, childType,
1158 |             "undefined has no properties", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1159 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1160 |       return;
1161 |     }
1162 | 
1163 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1164 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1165 |   }

/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.

   * @see #analyze()
   */

===== M0341 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1443-1482
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1443 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1444 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1445 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1446 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1447 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1448 |       // {?} - right curly
1449 |       // {?=} - equals
1450 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1451 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1452 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1453 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1454 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1455 |       token = next();
1456 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1457 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1458 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1459 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1460 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1461 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1462 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1463 |       }
1464 | 
1465 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1466 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1467 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1468 |     } else {
1469 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1470 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1471 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1472 |           next();
1473 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1474 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1475 |           next();
1476 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1477 |         }
1478 |       }
1479 | 
1480 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1481 |     }
1482 |   }

===== M0344 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1329-1364
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1329 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1330 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1331 | 
1332 |     skipEOLs();
1333 | 
1334 |     boolean restArg = false;
1335 |     token = next();
1336 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1337 |       token = next();
1338 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1339 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1340 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1341 |       }
1342 |       restArg = true;
1343 |     }
1344 | 
1345 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1346 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1347 |       skipEOLs();
1348 |       if (restArg) {
1349 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1350 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1351 |         next();
1352 |         skipEOLs();
1353 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1354 |       }
1355 | 
1356 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1357 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1358 |       } else {
1359 |         next();
1360 |       }
1361 |     }
1362 | 
1363 |     return typeNode;
1364 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
909 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
910 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
911 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
912 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
913 | 
914 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
915 |     int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
916 | 
917 |     jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno, endCharno,
918 |         true);
919 |     return typeNode;
920 |   }

Original range: 1315-1464

/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope, boolean) */
Method code:
1315 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1316 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope, boolean forgiving) {
1317 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1318 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1319 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1320 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1321 | 
1322 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1323 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1324 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, forgiving)
1325 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1326 | 
1327 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1328 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1329 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1330 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1331 |         }
1332 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1333 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1334 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope, forgiving));
1335 | 
1336 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1337 |         return createOptionalType(
1338 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1339 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1340 | 
1341 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1342 |         return createOptionalType(
1343 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1344 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1345 | 
1346 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1347 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1348 | 
1349 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1350 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1351 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1352 | 
1353 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1354 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1355 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1356 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1357 |           builder.addAlternate(
1358 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope, false));
1359 |         }
1360 |         return builder.build();
1361 | 
1362 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1363 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1364 | 
1365 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1366 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1367 | 
1368 |       case Token.STRING:
1369 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1370 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1371 |         if (forgiving) {
1372 |           namedType.forgiveUnknownNames();
1373 |         }
1374 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1375 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1376 |         }
1377 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1378 |             !(enumTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1379 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1380 |           if (typeList != null &&
1381 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1382 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1383 |             JSType parameterType =
1384 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1385 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1386 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1387 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1388 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1389 |               JSType indexType =
1390 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1391 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false);
1392 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1393 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1394 |             }
1395 |           }
1396 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1397 |         } else {
1398 |           return namedType;
1399 |         }
1400 | 
1401 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1402 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1403 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1404 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS) {
1405 |           Node thisNode = current.getFirstChild();
1406 |           thisType =
1407 |               ObjectType.cast(
1408 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1409 |                       thisNode, sourceName, scope, false)
1410 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1411 |           if (thisType == null) {
1412 |             reporter.warning(
1413 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject"),
1414 |                 sourceName, thisNode.getLineno(), "", thisNode.getCharno());
1415 |           }
1416 | 
1417 |           current = current.getNext();
1418 |         }
1419 | 
1420 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1421 | 
1422 |         if (current.getType() == Token.LP) {
1423 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1424 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1425 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1426 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1427 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1428 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1429 |               } else {
1430 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1431 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1432 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope, false));
1433 |               }
1434 |             } else {
1435 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1436 |                   arg, sourceName, scope, false);
1437 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1438 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1439 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1440 |                   reporter.warning(
1441 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1442 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), "", arg.getCharno());
1443 |                 }
1444 |               } else {
1445 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1446 |               }
1447 |             }
1448 |           }
1449 |           current = current.getNext();
1450 |         }
1451 | 
1452 |         JSType returnType =
1453 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope, false);
1454 | 
1455 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1456 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1457 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1458 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1459 |             .build();
1460 |     }
1461 | 
1462 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1463 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1464 |   }

/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
311 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
312 |     if (c <= 127) {
313 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
314 |     } else {
315 |       return c == 0xA0
316 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
317 |     }

139 |   public static Node transformTree(AstRoot node,
140 |                                    String sourceString,
141 |                                    Config config,
142 |                                    ErrorReporter errorReporter) {
143 |     IRFactory irFactory = new IRFactory(sourceString, node.getSourceName(),
144 |         config, errorReporter);
145 |     Node irNode = irFactory.transform(node);
146 |     // @license text gets appended onto the fileLevelJsDocBuilder as found,
147 |     // and stored straight into the JSDocInfo for the root node.
148 |     Node.FileLevelJsDocBuilder fileLevelJsDocBuilder =
149 |         irNode.getJsDocBuilderForNode();
150 |     // fileOverviewInfo stores the last bit of fileoverview data we saw.
151 |     // We only permit one, so throwing away extras is fair.
152 |     // The fileOverviewInfo gets passed into parseJSDocInfo so that
153 |     // it can detect when multiple @fileoverviews exist in the same file.
154 |     JSDocInfo fileOverviewInfo = null;
155 |     if (node.getComments() != null) {
156 |       for (Comment comment : node.getComments()) {
157 |         if (comment.getCommentType() == JSDOC) {
158 |           JsDocInfoParser jsDocParser =
159 |               irFactory.createJsDocInfoParser(comment.getValue(),
160 |                   comment.getLineno(), comment.getAbsolutePosition(),
161 |                   fileLevelJsDocBuilder, fileOverviewInfo);
162 |           if (jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo() != fileOverviewInfo) {
163 |             fileOverviewInfo = jsDocParser.getFileOverviewJSDocInfo();
164 |           } else {
165 |             JSDocInfo info = jsDocParser.retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
166 |             if (info != null) {
167 |               irFactory.attachJsDoc(comment, info);
168 |             }
169 |           }
170 |         }
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       // Only after we've seen all @fileoverview entries, attach the
174 |       // last one to the root node, and copy the found license strings
175 |       // to that node.
176 |       if (fileOverviewInfo != null) {
177 |         if ((irNode.getJSDocInfo() != null) &&
178 |             (irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense() != null)) {
179 |           fileOverviewInfo.setLicense(irNode.getJSDocInfo().getLicense());
180 |         }
181 |         irNode.setJSDocInfo(fileOverviewInfo);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |     return irNode;
185 |   }
