===== M0363 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1678-1702
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1678 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1679 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1680 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1681 |             type instanceof FunctionType ||
1682 |             type.isUnknownType());
1683 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1684 |     if (info != null) {
1685 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1686 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1687 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1688 |         type = infoType;
1689 |       }
1690 | 
1691 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1692 |         String propName = n.getType() == Token.GETPROP ?
1693 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1694 |         compiler.report(
1695 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1696 |       }
1697 |     }
1698 | 
1699 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1700 |       n.setJSType(type);
1701 |     }
1702 |   }

===== M0369 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1364-1394
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1364 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1365 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1366 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1367 | 
1368 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1369 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1370 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1371 |       return;
1372 |     }
1373 | 
1374 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1375 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1376 |     if (childType instanceof FunctionType) {
1377 |       FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) childType;
1378 | 
1379 |       // Non-native constructors should never be called directly.
1380 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1381 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType()) {
1382 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1383 |       }
1384 | 
1385 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1386 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1387 |     } else {
1388 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1389 |     }
1390 | 
1391 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1392 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1393 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1394 |   }

===== M0382 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 631-670
Documentation:

Method code:
631 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
632 |     String varName = n.getString();
633 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
634 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
635 |     if (value != null) {
636 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
637 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
638 |           getJSType(value));
639 |       return scope;
640 |     } else {
641 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
642 |       if (var != null) {
643 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
644 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
645 | 
646 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
647 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
648 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
649 |         boolean unflowable =
650 |             isInferred && unflowableVarNames.contains(varName);
651 | 
652 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
653 |         // inferred variable.
654 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
655 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
656 |             isInferred &&
657 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
658 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
659 | 
660 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
661 |           type = var.getType();
662 |           if (type == null) {
663 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
664 |           }
665 |         }
666 |       }
667 |     }
668 |     n.setJSType(type);
669 |     return scope;
670 |   }

/**
   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */
Method code:
889 |   private static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
890 |     switch (n.getType()) {
891 |       case Token.CALL:
892 |       case Token.GETPROP:
893 |       case Token.GETELEM:
894 |       case Token.THROW:
895 |       case Token.NEW:
896 |       case Token.ASSIGN:

905 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
906 |         return true;
907 |       }
908 |     }
909 |     return false;

===== M0398 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1319-1321
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1319 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1320 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1321 |   }

===== M0412 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 671-768
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
671 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
672 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
673 |     /*
674 |      * This is the case where:
675 |      *
676 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
677 |      * the script.
678 |      *
679 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
680 |      * the caller of the function.
681 |      *
682 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
683 |      * back to the caller of the function.
684 |      *
685 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
686 |      * asked to traverse.
687 |      *
688 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
689 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
690 |      */
691 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
692 |     if (parent == null || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION ||
693 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
694 |       return null;
695 |     }
696 | 
697 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
698 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
699 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
700 |       case Token.IF:
701 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
702 |       case Token.CASE:
703 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
704 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
705 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
706 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
707 |           if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.CASE) {
708 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
709 |           } else if (parent.getNext().getType() == Token.DEFAULT) {
710 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
711 |           } else {
712 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
713 |           }
714 |         } else {
715 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
716 |         }
717 |         break;
718 |       case Token.FOR:
719 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
720 |           return parent;
721 |         } else {
722 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
723 |         }
724 |       case Token.WHILE:
725 |       case Token.DO:
726 |         return parent;
727 |       case Token.TRY:
728 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
729 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
730 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
731 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
732 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
733 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
734 |           }
735 |         // CATCH block.
736 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
737 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
738 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
739 |           } else {
740 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
741 |           }
742 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
743 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
744 |           if (cfa != null) {
745 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
746 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
747 |             }
748 |           }
749 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
750 |         }
751 |     }
752 | 
753 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
754 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
755 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
756 | 
757 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
758 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
759 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
760 |     }
761 | 
762 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
763 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
764 |     } else {
765 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
766 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
767 |     }
768 |   }

===== M0418 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverse(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 285-465
Documentation:

Method code:
285 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
286 |     switch (n.getType()) {
287 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
288 |         scope = traverseAssign(n, scope);
289 |         break;
290 | 
291 |       case Token.NAME:
292 |         scope = traverseName(n, scope);
293 |         break;
294 | 
295 |       case Token.GETPROP:
296 |         scope = traverseGetProp(n, scope);
297 |         break;
298 | 
299 |       case Token.AND:
300 |         scope = traverseAnd(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
301 |             .createChildFlowScope();
302 |         break;
303 | 
304 |       case Token.OR:
305 |         scope = traverseOr(n, scope).getJoinedFlowScope()
306 |             .createChildFlowScope();
307 |         break;
308 | 
309 |       case Token.HOOK:
310 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
311 |         break;
312 | 
313 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
314 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
315 |         break;
316 | 
317 |       case Token.CALL:
318 |         scope = traverseCall(n, scope);
319 |         break;
320 | 
321 |       case Token.NEW:
322 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
323 |         break;
324 | 
325 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
326 |       case Token.ADD:
327 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
328 |         break;
329 | 
330 |       case Token.POS:
331 |       case Token.NEG:
332 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
333 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
334 |         break;
335 | 
336 |       case Token.NULL:
337 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NULL_TYPE));
338 |         break;
339 | 
340 |       case Token.VOID:
341 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
342 |         break;
343 | 
344 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
345 |         scope = traverseArrayLiteral(n, scope);
346 |         break;
347 | 
348 |       case Token.REF_SPECIAL:
349 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
350 |         break;
351 | 
352 |       case Token.REGEXP:
353 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(REGEXP_TYPE));
354 |         break;
355 | 
356 |       case Token.THIS:
357 |         n.setJSType(scope.getTypeOfThis());
358 |         break;
359 | 
360 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
361 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
362 |       case Token.LSH:
363 |       case Token.RSH:
364 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
365 |       case Token.URSH:
366 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
367 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
368 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
369 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
370 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
371 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
372 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
373 |       case Token.DIV:
374 |       case Token.MOD:
375 |       case Token.BITAND:
376 |       case Token.BITXOR:
377 |       case Token.BITOR:
378 |       case Token.MUL:
379 |       case Token.SUB:
380 |       case Token.DEC:
381 |       case Token.INC:
382 |       case Token.BITNOT:
383 |       case Token.NUMBER:
384 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
385 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));
386 |         break;
387 | 
388 |       case Token.LP:
389 |       case Token.GET_REF:
390 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
391 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
392 |         break;
393 | 
394 |       case Token.COMMA:
395 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
396 |         n.setJSType(getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
397 |         break;
398 | 
399 |       case Token.STRING:
400 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
401 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
402 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
403 |         break;
404 | 
405 |       case Token.LT:
406 |       case Token.LE:
407 |       case Token.GT:
408 |       case Token.GE:
409 |       case Token.NOT:
410 |       case Token.EQ:
411 |       case Token.NE:
412 |       case Token.SHEQ:
413 |       case Token.SHNE:
414 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
415 |       case Token.IN:
416 |       case Token.TRUE:
417 |       case Token.FALSE:
418 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
419 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE));
420 |         break;
421 | 
422 |       case Token.GETELEM:
423 |         scope = traverseGetElem(n, scope);
424 |         break;
425 | 
426 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
427 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
428 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
429 |           ensurePropertyDeclared(n.getFirstChild());
430 |         }
431 |         break;
432 | 
433 |       case Token.SWITCH:
434 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);
435 |         break;
436 | 
437 |       case Token.VAR:
438 |       case Token.RETURN:
439 |       case Token.THROW:
440 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
441 |         break;
442 | 
443 |       case Token.CATCH:
444 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
445 |         break;
446 |     }
447 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
448 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
449 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
450 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
451 | 
452 |         // A stubbed type cast on a qualified name should take
453 |         // effect for all subsequent accesses of that name,
454 |         // so treat it the same as an assign to that name.
455 |         if (n.isQualifiedName() &&
456 |             n.getParent().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
457 |           updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType(), castType);
458 |         }
459 | 
460 |         n.setJSType(castType);
461 |       }
462 |     }
463 | 
464 |     return scope;
465 |   }

Method code:
407 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
408 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
409 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
410 |     switch (n.getType()) {
411 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
412 |         // normal type checking
413 |         final TypeCheck outerThis = this;
414 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
415 |         final FunctionType functionType = (FunctionType) n.getJSType();
416 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
417 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
418 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
419 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
420 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
421 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
422 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
423 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
424 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
425 |         }
426 | 
427 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
428 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
429 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
430 |         break;
431 |     }
432 |     return true;
433 |   }

===== M0461 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.LocalScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1368-1393
Documentation:
/**
     * Visit a node in a local scope, and add any local variables or catch
     * parameters into the local symbol table.
     *
     * @param t The node traversal.
     * @param n The node being visited.
     * @param parent The parent of n
     */
Method code:
1368 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1369 |       if (n == scope.getRootNode()) return;
1370 | 
1371 |       if (n.getType() == Token.LP && parent == scope.getRootNode()) {
1372 |         handleFunctionInputs(parent);
1373 |         return;
1374 |       }
1375 | 
1376 |       switch (n.getType()) {
1377 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
1378 |           // VARs and ASSIGNs are handled separately.
1379 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
1380 |             return;
1381 |           }
1382 |           defineDeclaredFunction(n, parent);
1383 |           break;
1384 | 
1385 |         case Token.CATCH:
1386 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
1387 |           break;
1388 | 
1389 |         case Token.VAR:
1390 |           defineVar(n, parent);
1391 |           break;
1392 |       }
1393 |     }

===== M0463 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 149-194
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
149 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
150 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
151 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
152 |       case Token.BLOCK:
153 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
154 |       case Token.TRY:
155 |       case Token.FINALLY:
156 |         return true;
157 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
158 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
159 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
160 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
161 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
162 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
163 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
164 |         // child.
165 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
166 |       case Token.WHILE:
167 |       case Token.DO:
168 |       case Token.IF:
169 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
170 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
171 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
172 | 
173 |       case Token.FOR:
174 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
175 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
176 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
177 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
178 |         // That way the following:
179 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
180 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
181 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
182 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
183 |         } else {
184 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
185 |         }
186 |       case Token.SWITCH:
187 |       case Token.CASE:
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.WITH:
190 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
191 |       default:
192 |         return false;
193 |     }
194 |   }

===== M0466 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.buildAndRegister()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 546-584
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
546 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
547 |     if (returnType == null) {
548 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
549 |     }
550 | 
551 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
552 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
553 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
554 |     }
555 | 
556 |     FunctionType fnType;
557 |     if (isConstructor) {
558 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
559 |     } else if (isInterface) {
560 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(fnName, sourceNode);
561 |       if (scope.isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
562 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
563 |       }
564 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
565 |     } else {
566 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
567 |           .withName(fnName)
568 |           .withSourceNode(sourceNode)
569 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
570 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
571 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
572 |           .withTemplateName(templateTypeName)
573 |           .build();

575 |     }
576 |

578 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
579 |     }
580 | 
581 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeName();
582 | 
583 |     return fnType;
584 |   }

Original range: 1148-1156
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1148 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1149 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1150 |       return -1;
1151 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1152 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1153 |     } else {
1154 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1155 |     }
1156 |   }

===== M0486 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 145-186
Documentation:

Method code:
145 |   @Override
146 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
147 |     this.root = root;
148 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
149 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
150 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
151 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities);
152 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
153 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
154 | 
155 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
156 |     // search on the CFG.
157 |     priorityCounter = 0;
158 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
159 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
160 | 
161 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
162 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
163 |       // priority of them too.
164 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
165 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
166 |         if (value != null && value.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
167 |           Preconditions.checkState(
168 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
169 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
170 |         }
171 |       }
172 |     }
173 | 
174 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
175 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
176 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
177 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
178 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
179 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
180 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
181 |       }
182 |     }
183 | 
184 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
185 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
186 |   }

===== M0501 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.extractBlockComment(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1214-1259
Documentation:
/**
   * Extracts the top-level block comment from the JsDoc comment, if any.
   * This method differs from the extractMultilineTextualBlock in that it
   * terminates under different conditions (it doesn't have the same
   * prechecks), it does not first read in the remaining of the current
   * line and its conditions for ignoring the "*" (STAR) are different.
   *
   * @param token The starting token.
   *
   * @return The extraction information.
   */
Method code:
1214 |   private ExtractionInfo extractBlockComment(JsDocToken token) {
1215 |     StringBuilder builder = new StringBuilder();
1216 | 
1217 |     boolean ignoreStar = true;
1218 | 
1219 |     do {
1220 |       switch (token) {
1221 |         case ANNOTATION:
1222 |         case EOC:
1223 |         case EOF:
1224 |           return new ExtractionInfo(builder.toString().trim(), token);
1225 | 
1226 |         case STAR:
1227 |           if (!ignoreStar) {
1228 |             if (builder.length() > 0) {
1229 |               builder.append(' ');
1230 |             }
1231 | 
1232 |             builder.append('*');
1233 |           }
1234 | 
1235 |           token = next();
1236 |           continue;
1237 | 
1238 |         case EOL:
1239 |           ignoreStar = true;
1240 |           builder.append('\n');
1241 |           token = next();
1242 |           continue;
1243 | 
1244 |         default:
1245 |           if (!ignoreStar && builder.length() > 0) {
1246 |             builder.append(' ');
1247 |           }
1248 | 
1249 |           ignoreStar = false;
1250 | 
1251 |           builder.append(toString(token));
1252 | 
1253 |           String line = stream.getRemainingJSDocLine();
1254 |           line = trimEnd(line);
1255 |           builder.append(line);
1256 |           token = next();
1257 |       }
1258 |     } while (true);
1259 |   }

===== M0503 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.transform(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 203-233
Documentation:

Method code:
203 |   private Node transform(AstNode node) {
204 |     String jsDoc = node.getJsDoc();
205 |     NodeWithJsDoc nodeWithJsDoc = null;
206 |     if (jsDoc != null) {
207 |       nodeWithJsDoc = new NodeWithJsDoc();
208 |       nodesWithJsDoc.put(jsDoc, nodeWithJsDoc);
209 |     }
210 | 
211 |     Node irNode = justTransform(node);
212 |     if (nodeWithJsDoc != null) {
213 |       nodeWithJsDoc.node = irNode;
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
217 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
218 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
219 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
220 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
221 |     } else {
222 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
223 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
224 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
225 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
226 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
227 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
228 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
229 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
230 |       }
231 |     }
232 |     return irNode;
233 |   }

===== M0528 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 441-478
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
441 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
442 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
443 |     int type = n.getType();
444 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
445 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
446 |     }
447 | 
448 |     curNode = n;
449 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
450 | 
451 |     switch (type) {
452 |       case Token.CATCH:
453 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
454 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
455 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
456 |         // is the code block
457 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild(), n);
458 |         traverseBranch(n.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext(), n);
459 |         break;
460 | 
461 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
462 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
463 |         break;
464 | 
465 |       default:
466 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
467 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
468 |           // would no longer point to the true next
469 |           Node next = child.getNext();
470 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
471 |           child = next;
472 |         }
473 |         break;
474 |     }
475 | 
476 |     curNode = n;
477 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
478 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.getMinArguments()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 212-225
Documentation:
/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */
Method code:
212 |   public int getMinArguments() {
213 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
214 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position
215 |     // of the last required parameter.
216 |     int i = 0;
217 |     int min = 0;
218 |     for (Node n : getParameters()) {
219 |       i++;
220 |       if (!n.isOptionalArg() && !n.isVarArgs()) {
221 |         min = i;
222 |       }
223 |     }
224 |     return min;
225 |   }

===== M0534 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 316-324
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
316 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
317 |       Node ret = transform(n);
318 |       if (ret.getType() == Token.STRING) {
319 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
320 |       } else if (ret.getType() == Token.NAME) {
321 |         ret.setType(Token.STRING);
322 |       }
323 |       return ret;
324 |     }

===== M0540 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 463-518
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   */
Method code:
463 |   void expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, Node n, Node parent, Var var,
464 |       String variableName, JSType newType) {
465 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
466 |     if (n.getType() == Token.GETPROP) {
467 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
468 |       if (info == null) {
469 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
470 |       }
471 |       allowDupe =
472 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
476 | 
477 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
478 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
479 |     // compilation process.
480 |     if (varType != null &&
481 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
482 |         newType != null &&
483 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
484 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
485 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
486 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
487 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
488 |       // native type.
489 |       if (var.input == null) {
490 |         n.setJSType(varType);
491 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
492 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
493 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
494 |           }
495 |         } else {
496 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
497 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
498 |         }
499 |       } else {
500 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
501 |         // match the original type.
502 |         //
503 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
504 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
505 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
506 |               var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) ||
507 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
508 |           if (shouldReport) {
509 |             compiler.report(
510 |                 JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
511 |                     variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
512 |                     String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
513 |                     varType.toString()));
514 |           }
515 |         }
516 |       }
517 |     }
518 |   }

===== M0552 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 152-190
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
152 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
153 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
154 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
155 |     // show up in the type registry.
156 |     Scope newScope = null;
157 |     if (parent == null) {
158 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
159 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
160 | 
161 |       GlobalScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
162 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
163 |       scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
164 |       scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
165 | 
166 |       // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
167 |       // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
168 |       // build properties on.
169 |       for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
170 |         JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
171 |         if (type != null && type instanceof FunctionType) {
172 |           FunctionType fnType = (FunctionType) type;
173 |           ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
174 |           if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
175 |             NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
176 |                 scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
177 |           }
178 |         }
179 |       }
180 | 
181 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
182 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes);
183 |     } else {
184 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
185 |       LocalScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
186 |       scopeBuilder.build();
187 |       scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
188 |     }
189 |     return newScope;
190 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)

Original range: 1520-1542
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1520 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1521 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1522 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1523 |     }
1524 | 
1525 |     Node typeName = newStringNode(stream.getString());
1526 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1527 |       next();
1528 |       skipEOLs();
1529 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1530 |       if (memberType != null) {
1531 |         typeName.addChildToFront(memberType);
1532 | 
1533 |         skipEOLs();
1534 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1535 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1536 |         }
1537 | 
1538 |         next();
1539 |       }
1540 |     }
1541 |     return typeName;
1542 |   }

===== M0560 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 547-589
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
547 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
548 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
549 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
550 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
551 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
552 |     if (objectType == null) {
553 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
554 |     } else {
555 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
556 |         return;
557 |       }
558 | 
559 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
560 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
561 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
562 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
563 |         //
564 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
565 |         //    inferred type.
566 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
567 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
568 |         //    define it.
569 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
570 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
571 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
572 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
573 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
574 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
575 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
576 |           } else {
577 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
578 |           }
579 |         } else {
580 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.THIS &&
581 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
582 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(propName, rightType, false);
583 |           } else {
584 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
585 |           }
586 |         }
587 |       }
588 |     }
589 |   }

===== M0562 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.GlobalScopeBuilder.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 850-909
Documentation:
/**
     * Visit a node in the global scope, and add anything it declares to the
     * global symbol table.
     *
     * @param t The current traversal.
     * @param n The node being visited.
     * @param parent The parent of n
     */
Method code:
850 |     @Override public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
851 |       switch (n.getType()) {
852 |         case Token.CALL:
853 |           checkForClassDefiningCalls(t, n, parent);
854 |           break;
855 | 
856 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
857 |           if (!t.getInput().isExtern()) {
858 |             nonExternFunctions.add(n);
859 |           }
860 | 
861 |           // VARs and ASSIGNs are handled separately.
862 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN ||
863 |               parent.getType() == Token.NAME) {
864 |             return;
865 |           }
866 | 
867 |           defineDeclaredFunction(n, parent);
868 |           break;
869 | 
870 |         case Token.ASSIGN:
871 |           // Handle constructor and enum definitions.
872 |           defineNamedTypeAssign(n, parent);
873 | 
874 |           // Handle typedefs.
875 |           checkForOldStyleTypedef(t, n);
876 | 
877 |           // Handle initialization of properties.
878 |           Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
879 |           if (firstChild.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
880 |               firstChild.isQualifiedName()) {
881 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(),
882 |                 firstChild, n, firstChild.getNext());
883 |           }
884 |           break;
885 | 
886 |         case Token.CATCH:
887 |           defineCatch(n, parent);
888 |           break;
889 | 
890 |         case Token.VAR:
891 |           defineVar(n, parent);
892 | 
893 |           // Handle typedefs.
894 |           if (n.hasOneChild()) {
895 |             checkForOldStyleTypedef(t, n);
896 |             checkForTypedef(t, n.getFirstChild(), n.getJSDocInfo());
897 |           }
898 |           break;
899 | 
900 |         case Token.GETPROP:
901 |           // Handle stubbed properties.
902 |           if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT &&
903 |               n.isQualifiedName()) {
904 |             checkForTypedef(t, n, n.getJSDocInfo());
905 |             maybeDeclareQualifiedName(t, n.getJSDocInfo(), n, parent, null);
906 |           }
907 |           break;
908 |       }
909 |     }

172 |   FlowScope flowThrough(Node n, FlowScope input) {

===== M0582 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 777-791
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
777 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
778 |     if (item == null) {
779 |       return null;
780 |     } else if (item.type == propType) {
781 |       return item.next;
782 |     } else {
783 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.next, propType);
784 |       if (result != item.next) {
785 |         return new PropListItem(
786 |             item.type, item.intValue, item.objectValue, result);
787 |       } else {
788 |         return item;
789 |       }
790 |     }
791 |   }

===== M0601 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isStatement(Node)

/**
   * @return Whether the node is used as a statement.
   */
Method code:
1138 |   static boolean isStatement(Node n) {
1139 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
1140 |     // It is not possible to determine definitely if a node is a statement
1141 |     // or not if it is not part of the AST.  A FUNCTION node can be
1142 |     // either part of an expression or a statement.
1143 |     Preconditions.checkState(parent != null);
1144 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
1145 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
1146 |       case Token.BLOCK:
1147 |       case Token.LABEL:
1148 |         return true;
1149 |       default:
1150 |         return false;
1151 |     }
1152 |   }

===== M0616 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 338-392
Documentation:

Method code:
338 |   protected int getChar() {
339 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
340 |       cursor++;
341 |       --ungetCursor;
342 |       if (charno == -1) {
343 |         charno = getOffset();
344 |       }
345 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
346 |     }
347 | 
348 |     for(;;) {
349 |       int c;
350 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
351 |         hitEOF = true;
352 |         if (charno == -1) {
353 |           charno = getOffset();
354 |         }
355 |         return EOF_CHAR;
356 |       }
357 |       cursor++;
358 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
359 | 
360 | 
361 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
362 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
363 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
364 |           continue;
365 |         }
366 |         lineEndChar = -1;
367 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
368 |         lineno++;
369 |       }
370 | 
371 |       if (c <= 127) {
372 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
373 |           lineEndChar = c;
374 |           c = '\n';
375 |         }
376 |       } else {
377 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
378 |           continue;
379 |         }
380 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
381 |           lineEndChar = c;
382 |           c = '\n';
383 |         }
384 |       }
385 | 
386 |       if (charno == -1) {
387 |         charno = getOffset();
388 |       }
389 | 
390 |       return c;
391 |     }
392 |   }

===== M0619 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.getPropertyType(JSType,String,Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 1032-1066
Documentation:

Method code:
1032 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1033 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1034 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1035 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1036 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1037 |     if (var != null) {
1038 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1039 |       if (varType != null) {
1040 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1041 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1042 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1043 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1044 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1045 |         } else {
1046 |           return varType;
1047 |         }
1048 |       }
1049 |     }
1050 | 
1051 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1052 |     if (objType != null) {
1053 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1054 |     }
1055 | 
1056 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
1057 |         qualifiedName != null) {
1058 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1059 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1060 |       if (regType != null) {
1061 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1062 |       }
1063 |     }
1064 | 
1065 |     return propertyType;
1066 |   }

===== M0620 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 80-114
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
80 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
81 |                            String sourceString,
82 |                            Config config,
83 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
84 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
85 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
86 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
87 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
88 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
89 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
90 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
91 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
92 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(true);
93 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
94 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
95 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
96 |     }
97 | 
98 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
99 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
100 |     try {
101 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
102 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
103 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
104 |     } finally {
105 |       Context.exit();
106 |     }
107 |     Node root = null;
108 |     if (astRoot != null) {
109 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
110 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
111 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
112 |     }
113 |     return root;
114 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java

/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
457 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
458 |     {
459 |         final String defaultResource
460 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
461 | 
462 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
463 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
464 | 
465 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
466 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
467 | 
468 |         String formatString;
469 |         try {
470 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
471 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
472 |             throw new RuntimeException
473 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
474 |         }
475 | 
476 |         /*
477 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
478 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
479 |          * single 's.
480 |          */
481 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
482 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
483 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
484 |     }

812 | 
813 |     if (scope != null && scope.getParentScope() == null) {
814 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.
815 |       // If the user has defined a Window type, make the Window the
816 |       // implicit prototype of "this".
817 |       PrototypeObjectType globalThis = (PrototypeObjectType) getNativeType(
818 |           JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
819 |       JSType windowType = getType("Window");
820 |       if (globalThis.isUnknownType()) {
821 |         ObjectType windowObjType = ObjectType.cast(windowType);
822 |         if (windowObjType != null) {
823 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(windowObjType);
824 |         } else {
825 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(
826 |               getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE));
827 |         }
828 |       }
829 |     }
830 |   }

===== M0631 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 483-518
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
483 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
484 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
485 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
486 | 
487 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
488 | 
489 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
490 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
491 | 
492 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
493 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
494 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
495 |     }
496 | 
497 |     curNode = n;
498 |     pushScope(n);
499 | 
500 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
501 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
502 |       // scope.
503 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
504 |     }
505 | 
506 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
507 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
508 | 
509 |     // Args
510 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
511 | 
512 |     // Body
513 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
514 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
515 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
516 | 
517 |     popScope();
518 |   }

===== M0663 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 775-789
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
775 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
776 |     switch (n.getType()) {
777 |       case Token.DO:
778 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
779 |       case Token.FOR:
780 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
781 |           return n;
782 |         }
783 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
784 |       case Token.LABEL:
785 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
786 |       default:
787 |         return n;
788 |     }
789 |   }

/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string
   *         of the name and properties.
   */
Method code:
1728 |   public String getQualifiedName() {
1729 |     if (type == Token.NAME) {
1730 |       return getString();
1731 |     } else if (type == Token.GETPROP) {
1732 |       String left = getFirstChild().getQualifiedName();
1733 |       if (left == null) {
1734 |         return null;
1735 |       }
1736 |       return left + "." + getLastChild().getString();
1737 |     } else if (type == Token.THIS) {
1738 |       return "this";
1739 |     } else {
1740 |       return null;
1741 |     }
1742 |   }

===== M0676 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 252-302
Documentation:

Method code:
252 |   @Override
253 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
254 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
255 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
256 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
257 |         return true;
258 |       }
259 | 
260 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
261 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
262 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
263 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
264 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
265 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
266 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
267 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
268 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
269 |         return false;
270 |       }
271 | 
272 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
273 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
274 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
275 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
276 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
277 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
278 |             return false;
279 |           }
280 |         }
281 | 
282 |         return true;
283 |       }
284 | 
285 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
286 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
287 | 
288 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
289 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
290 |           return false;
291 |         }
292 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
293 |       }
294 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
295 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
296 |           return false;
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |       return true;
300 |     }
301 |     return false;
302 |   }

===== M0681 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 478-517
Documentation:

Method code:
478 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
479 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
480 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
481 |     if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && parent != null &&
482 |         parent.getType() == Token.TRY &&
483 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
484 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
485 |       return;
486 |     }
487 | 
488 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
489 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
490 | 
491 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
492 |     // function (unless it is called)
493 |     while (child != null && child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
494 |       child = child.getNext();
495 |     }
496 | 
497 |     if (child != null) {
498 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
499 |     } else {
500 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
501 |     }
502 | 
503 |     // Synthetic blocks
504 |     if (parent != null) {
505 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
506 |         case Token.DEFAULT:
507 |         case Token.CASE:
508 |         case Token.TRY:
509 |           break;
510 |         default:
511 |           if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
512 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
513 |           }
514 |           break;
515 |       }
516 |     }
517 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 511-541
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
511 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
512 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
513 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
514 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
515 |     if (isOptional) {
516 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
517 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
518 |       // error can be reported.
519 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
520 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
521 |         emittedWarning = true;
522 |       }
523 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
524 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
525 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
526 |         emittedWarning = true;
527 |       }
528 |     } else {
529 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
530 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
531 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
532 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
533 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
534 |         } else {
535 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
536 |         }
537 |         emittedWarning = true;
538 |       }
539 |     }
540 |     return emittedWarning;
541 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 879-955
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
879 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
880 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
881 | 
882 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
883 |       return;
884 |     }
885 | 
886 |     int i = 0;
887 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
888 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
889 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
890 |       JSType iParameterType = getJSType(iParameter);
891 |       iParameterType = iParameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
892 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
893 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
894 |         if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
895 |           // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
896 |           return;
897 |         }
898 |         Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
899 |         JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
900 |         if (iArgumentType != null) {
901 |           iArgumentType = iArgumentType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
902 |           if (!(iArgumentType instanceof ObjectType)) {
903 |             compiler.report(
904 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
905 |                              TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE));
906 |             return;
907 |           }
908 | 
909 |           // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
910 |           boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
911 |           int j = 0;
912 |           for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
913 |             JSType jParameterType = getJSType(jParameter);
914 |             if (jParameterType instanceof FunctionType) {
915 |               FunctionType jParameterFnType = (FunctionType) jParameterType;
916 |               if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
917 |                 foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
918 |                 // Find the actual type of this argument.
919 |                 if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
920 |                   // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
921 |                   return;
922 |                 }
923 |                 Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
924 |                 JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
925 |                 if (jArgument.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
926 |                     jArgumentType instanceof FunctionType) {
927 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
928 |                   // using the actual type of T.
929 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType =(FunctionType) jArgumentType;
930 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
931 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
932 |                     // function.
933 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
934 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
935 |                             jArgumentFnType, (ObjectType) iArgumentType));
936 |                   }
937 |                 }
938 |                 // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
939 |                 // types of the arguments match.
940 |               }
941 |             }
942 |             j++;
943 |           }
944 | 
945 |           if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
946 |             Node source = fnType.getSource();
947 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(source), source,
948 |                                          TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
949 |             return;
950 |           }
951 |         }
952 |       }
953 |       i++;
954 |     }
955 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.TypeInference(AbstractCompiler,ControlFlowGraph<Node>,ReverseAbstractInterpreter,Scope,Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec>,Collection<Var>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 111-151
Documentation:
// TODO(nicksantos): Create a builder for this class.
Method code:
111 |   TypeInference(AbstractCompiler compiler, ControlFlowGraph<Node> cfg,
112 |                 ReverseAbstractInterpreter reverseInterpreter,
113 |                 Scope functionScope,
114 |                 Map<String, AssertionFunctionSpec> assertionFunctionsMap,
115 |                 Collection<Var> unflowableVars) {
116 |     super(cfg, new LinkedFlowScope.FlowScopeJoinOp());
117 |     this.compiler = compiler;
118 |     this.registry = compiler.getTypeRegistry();
119 |     this.reverseInterpreter = reverseInterpreter;
120 |     this.syntacticScope = functionScope;
121 |     this.functionScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(functionScope);
122 |     this.assertionFunctionsMap = assertionFunctionsMap;
123 | 
124 |     for (Var unflowableVar : unflowableVars) {
125 |       String name = unflowableVar.getName();
126 |       if (functionScope.getVar(name) == unflowableVar) {
127 |         this.unflowableVarNames.add(name);
128 |       }
129 |     }
130 | 
131 |     Iterator<Var> varIt = functionScope.getVars();
132 |     while (varIt.hasNext()) {
133 |       Var var = varIt.next();
134 |       if (this.unflowableVarNames.contains(var.getName())) {
135 |         continue;
136 |       }
137 | 
138 |       // For each local variable declared with the VAR keyword, the entry
139 |       // type is VOID.
140 |       if (var.getParentNode() != null &&
141 |           var.getType() == null && // no declared type
142 |           var.getParentNode().getType() == Token.VAR &&
143 |           !var.isExtern()) {
144 |         this.functionScope.inferSlotType(
145 |             var.getName(), getNativeType(VOID_TYPE));
146 |       }
147 |     }
148 | 
149 |     this.bottomScope = LinkedFlowScope.createEntryLattice(
150 |         new Scope(functionScope.getRootNode(), functionScope.getTypeOfThis()));
151 |   }

===== M0717 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 406-450
Documentation:

Method code:
406 |   @Override
407 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
408 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
409 |       return true;
410 |     }
411 | 
412 |     // Union types
413 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
414 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
415 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
416 |       return false;
417 |     }
418 | 
419 |     // record types
420 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
421 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
422 |     }
423 | 
424 |     // Interfaces
425 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
426 |     // to the interface instance.
427 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
428 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
429 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
430 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
431 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
432 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
433 |           return true;
434 |         }
435 |       }
436 |     }
437 | 
438 |     // other prototype based objects
439 |     if (that != null) {
440 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
441 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
442 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
443 |         // to avoid guessing.
444 |         return true;
445 |       }
446 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     return false;
450 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferParameterTypes(Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 390-459
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
390 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
391 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
392 |     if (argsParent == null) {
393 |       if (info == null) {
394 |         return this;
395 |       } else {
396 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
397 |       }
398 |     }
399 | 
400 |     // arguments
401 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
402 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
403 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
404 |     }
405 | 
406 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
407 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
408 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
409 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
410 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
411 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
412 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
413 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
414 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
415 | 
416 |       // type from JSDocInfo
417 |       JSType parameterType = null;
418 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
419 |       boolean isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
420 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
421 |         parameterType =
422 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
423 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
424 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
425 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
426 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
427 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
428 |       } else {
429 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
430 |       }
431 | 
432 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
433 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
434 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
435 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
436 |         }
437 |         foundTemplateType = true;
438 |       }
439 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
440 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
441 |           isOptionalParam,
442 |           isVarArgs);
443 | 
444 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
445 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
446 |       }
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
450 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
451 |     }
452 | 
453 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
454 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
455 |     }
456 | 
457 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
458 |     return this;
459 |   }
