Original range: 421-447
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
421 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
422 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
423 | 
424 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
425 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
426 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
427 |       return 0;
428 |     }
429 | 
430 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
431 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
432 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than extraTracingStatistics.size() because
433 |       // a new statistic may have been added
434 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
435 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
436 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
437 |       }
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
441 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
442 |       return 0;
443 |     }
444 | 
445 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
446 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
447 |   }

===== M0763 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.GlobalScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1098-1198
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1098 |     private void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1099 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1100 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1101 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1102 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1103 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1104 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1105 | 
1106 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1107 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1108 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1109 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1110 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1111 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1112 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1113 |         if (qVar != null) {
1114 |           if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1115 |             // Just ignore assigns to declared prototypes.
1116 |             return;
1117 |           }
1118 |           scope.undeclare(qVar);
1119 |         }
1120 |       }
1121 | 
1122 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1123 |       // 1) @type annotation / @enum annotation
1124 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1125 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1126 |       // 4) ASSIGN to anything else
1127 |       //
1128 |       // 1 and 3 are declarations, 4 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1129 |       // the function has not been declared before.
1130 |       //
1131 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1132 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1133 | 
1134 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3
1135 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredGetPropType(t, info, n, rhsValue);
1136 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1137 |         // Determining type for #4
1138 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1139 |       }
1140 | 
1141 |       if (valueType == null) {
1142 |         if (parent.getType() == Token.EXPR_RESULT) {
1143 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1144 |               n, t.getInput().isExtern(), ownerName));
1145 |         } else if (rhsValue != null &&
1146 |                    rhsValue.getType() == Token.TRUE) {
1147 |           // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1148 |           ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1149 |           if (ownerType instanceof FunctionType) {
1150 |             JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ((FunctionType) ownerType).getTypeOfThis();
1151 |             String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1152 |             JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1153 |                 null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1154 |             if (delegateType != null &&
1155 |                 ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1156 |               defineSlot(n, parent, typeRegistry.getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE),
1157 |                          true);
1158 |             }
1159 |           }
1160 |         }
1161 | 
1162 |         return;
1163 |       }
1164 | 
1165 |       boolean inferred = true;
1166 |       if (info != null) {
1167 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3
1168 |         inferred = !(info.hasType() || info.hasEnumParameterType() ||
1169 |             FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1170 |       }
1171 | 
1172 |       if (inferred) {
1173 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1174 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1175 |             rhsValue.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
1176 |             !scope.isDeclared(qName, false));
1177 |       }
1178 | 
1179 |       if (!inferred) {
1180 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1181 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1182 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1183 |           // declared yet.
1184 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput().isExtern();
1185 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1186 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1187 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1188 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1189 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1190 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, isExtern);
1191 |           }
1192 |         }
1193 | 
1194 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1195 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1196 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1197 |       }
1198 |     }

===== M0777 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.GlobalScopeBuilder.checkForClassDefiningCalls(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 916-975
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
916 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
917 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
918 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
919 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
920 |       if (relationship != null) {
921 |         ObjectType superClass = ObjectType.cast(
922 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.superclassName));
923 |         ObjectType subClass = ObjectType.cast(
924 |             typeRegistry.getType(relationship.subclassName));
925 |         if (superClass != null && subClass != null) {
926 |           FunctionType superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
927 |           FunctionType subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
928 | 
929 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS) {
930 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
931 |           }
932 | 
933 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
934 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
935 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
936 |           }
937 |         }
938 |       }
939 | 
940 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
941 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
942 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
943 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
944 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
945 |         if (objectType != null) {
946 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
947 | 
948 |           if (functionType != null) {
949 |             FunctionType getterType =
950 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
951 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
952 |                 objectType);
953 |           }
954 |         }
955 |       }
956 | 
957 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
958 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
959 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
960 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
961 |       }
962 | 
963 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
964 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
965 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
966 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
967 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
968 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
969 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
970 |         } else {
971 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
972 |                   CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
973 |         }
974 |       }
975 |     }

===== M0787 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(CodingConvention,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2079-2090
Documentation:
/** Whether the given name is constant by coding convention. */
Method code:
2079 |   static boolean isConstantByConvention(
2080 |       CodingConvention convention, Node node, Node parent) {
2081 |     String name = node.getString();
2082 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
2083 |         node == parent.getLastChild()) {
2084 |       return convention.isConstantKey(name);
2085 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(node, parent)) {
2086 |       return convention.isConstantKey(name);
2087 |     } else {
2088 |       return convention.isConstant(name);
2089 |     }
2090 |   }
